      const turns=longTermRuntime.roomId===String(room.chatId)?longTermRuntime.turns:null;
      const index=turns&&cursor?turns.findIndex(turn=>String(turn.key)===cursor):-1;
      const longTermCount=turns&&(!cursor||index>=0)?turns.length-index-1:counts?.longterm?.anchor===cursor?Number(counts.longterm.count)||0:0;
      const amount=task==='longterm'?longTermCount:Number(counts?.[task]?.count)||0;
      const limit=Math.max(5,Math.min(1000,Number(center.areaTurns[task])||center.updateTurns));
      const alreadyWaiting=task==='longterm'?!!(pending||meta.drafts.externalPacket||meta.drafts.second):jobs.some(job=>job.task===task)||Object.entries(AI_MEMORY_AREAS).some(([area,meta])=>meta.manual===task&&aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[area].draft);
      if(center.areaEnabled[task]!==false&&amount>=limit&&!alreadyWaiting)
        noticeUpsert(room,{type:'update',title:`${info.title} 갱신을 권장해요`,message:`마지막 적용 이후 완료 RP가 ${amount}턴 쌓였어요. ${limit}턴 기준을 넘었습니다.`,targetArea:task==='longterm'?'longterm':'update',targetRef:task,dedupeKey:`update:${task}`});
      else noticeResolve(room,`update:${task}`);
    }
  }
  function noticeUnreadCount(room){
    const center=noticeState(room);
    return center.enabled?center.items.filter(item=>!item.resolved&&!item.read&&!noticePausedAiMemoryError(room,item)&&center.categories[item.type]!==false&&!(item.type==='update'&&center.areaEnabled[item.targetRef]===false)&&Number(item.snoozedUntil||0)<=Date.now()&&item.ignoredMessage!==item.message).length:0;
  }
  function renderNotificationPreferences(room){
    const center=noticeState(room);
    const categoryRows=Object.entries(NOTICE_CATEGORIES).map(([key,label])=>`<label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>${esc(label)}</span><input type="checkbox" data-pref-notice-category="${key}" ${center.categories[key]!==false?'checked':''}></label>`).join('');
    const areaRows=Object.entries(noticeUpdateAreas()).map(([key,info])=>`<label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>${esc(info.title)}</span><span class="rpcm-pref-controls"><input type="checkbox" data-pref-notice-area="${key}" aria-label="${esc(info.title)} 알림" ${center.areaEnabled[key]!==false?'checked':''}><input type="number" min="5" max="1000" data-pref-notice-turns="${key}" aria-label="${esc(info.title)} 갱신 권장 턴" value="${Number(center.areaTurns[key])||center.updateTurns}"><small>턴</small></span></label>`).join('');
    return `<div class="rpcm-settings-card" id="rpcm-notification-preferences"><h2>알림</h2><p>새 RP, AI 초안, 저장 오류와 장기기억 정리 제안을 알려줍니다. 알림을 꺼도 자동 작업 설정은 바뀌지 않습니다.</p><label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>알림센터 사용</span><input type="checkbox" data-pref-notice="enabled" ${center.enabled?'checked':''}></label><div class="rpcm-notice-pref-grid"><label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>갱신 권장 기준</span><span class="rpcm-pref-controls"><input type="number" min="5" max="1000" data-pref-notice="updateTurns" value="${center.updateTurns}"><small>턴</small></span></label><label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>장기기억 정리 제안</span><span class="rpcm-pref-controls"><input type="number" min="5" max="200" data-pref-notice="longTermLimit" value="${center.longTermLimit}"><small>개 초과</small></span></label></div><details><summary>종류·영역별 알림</summary><div class="rpcm-notice-pref-grid">${categoryRows}</div><div class="rpcm-notice-pref-grid rpcm-notice-area-grid">${areaRows}</div></details><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-pref-notice-open>알림 확인</button></div>`;
  }
  function longTermSecondEligible(room,inventory){
    const meta=longTermState(room),blocked=new Set([...meta.protectedIds,...meta.finalCompressedIds,...meta.nativeAuto.userModifiedIds].map(String));
    for(const source of [meta.firstStage.pendingApply,meta.nativeAuto.pendingApply]){
      for(const row of source?.source||[])blocked.add(String(row.id||''));
      for(const id of [...(source?.savedIds||[]),...(source?.existingIds||[]),...(source?.updateTargets||[])])if(id)blocked.add(String(id));
      for(const row of source?.operations||[])if(row?.id)blocked.add(String(row.id));
    }
    return inventory.filter(item=>{
      const id=longTermId(item),managed=meta.nativeAuto.managedHashes[id],known=meta.nativeAuto.nativeHashes[id],fingerprint=longTermFingerprint(item);
      return id&&!blocked.has(id)&&(!managed||managed===fingerprint)&&(!known||known===fingerprint);
    });
  }
  async function refreshLongTermNoticeInventory(room){
    if(!room||state.currentRoom!==room||!noticeState(room).enabled||noticeState(room).categories.longterm===false)return;
    state.noticeLongTermInventories ||= new Map();
    const key=String(room.chatId),cached=state.noticeLongTermInventories.get(key);
    if(cached&&Date.now()-cached.checkedAt<120000)return;
    try{
      const inventory=await longTermFetch(room);
      state.noticeLongTermInventories.set(key,{inventory,checkedAt:Date.now()});
      noticeReconcile(room);
    }catch(error){console.warn('[RP Manager] 장기기억 알림 확인 실패',error);}
  }
  function updateNoticeBadge(room){
    if(!room||state.currentRoom!==room)return;
    const badge=state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-notice-badge');
    if(badge){const count=noticeUnreadCount(room);badge.hidden=!count;badge.textContent=count>99?'99+':String(count);}
  }
  function ensureNoticeStyles(){
    if(document.getElementById('rpcm-notice-styles'))return;
    const style=document.createElement('style');style.id='rpcm-notice-styles';
    style.textContent=`#rpcm-notice-open{position:relative}#rpcm-notice-badge{position:absolute;right:1px;top:0;min-width:16px;padding:1px 3px;border-radius:20px;background:#c45e91;color:var(--wish-accent-ink);font:600 10px/15px sans-serif}
      #rpcm-notice-backdrop{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483605;background:var(--wish-scrim);display:grid;place-items:center;padding:18px}
      #rpcm-notice-dialog{width:min(680px,96vw);max-height:min(780px,90vh);overflow:auto;background:var(--wish-sheet);color:var(--wish-fg);border:1px solid var(--wish-line);border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 20px 70px rgba(38,23,32,.18);padding:20px}
      #rpcm-notice-dialog header{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;margin-bottom:12px}#rpcm-notice-dialog h2{font-size:19px;margin:0}

      #rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-tabs{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-bottom:12px}#rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-tabs button[aria-selected="true"]{background:var(--wish-accent-soft);color:var(--wish-accent-strong);border-color:var(--wish-accent-line)}

      #rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-row.is-unread{border-left:4px solid #c45e91}#rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-row p{margin:0;color:var(--wish-fg-2);line-height:1.5}
      #rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-row small{color:var(--wish-muted)}#rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-actions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:5px}
      #rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-settings{display:grid;gap:10px}#rpcm-notice-dialog .rpcm-notice-settings label{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}

    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }
  // Notice navigation opens a review or points at an existing action. It never generates or saves a result.
  let noticeGuidance = null, noticeNavigationEpoch = 0;
  function noticeClearGuidance(){
    noticeGuidance=null;
    document.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-notice-guidance').forEach(node=>node.remove());
    document.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-notice-next-action').forEach(node=>node.classList.remove('rpcm-notice-next-action'));
  }
  function noticeActionLabel(item){
    if(item.dedupeKey==='manual:waiting')return '대기 작업 이어가기';
    if(item.dedupeKey?.startsWith('manual:integrity:'))return '해당 저장본 점검';
    if(item.type==='draft')return '준비된 결과 검토';
    if(item.type==='manual')return '결과 가져오기';
    if(item.type==='update')return '갱신할 항목 보기';
    if(item.type==='refiner')return '해당 교정 기록 보기';
    if(item.type==='longterm')return '2차 정리할 기억 보기';
    if(item.type==='error')return '오류와 다음 단계 확인';
    return '확인할 항목 보기';
  }
  function noticeShowGuidance(room,item,options){
    if(state.currentRoom!==room)return;
    noticeClearGuidance();
    noticeGuidance={roomId:String(room.chatId),item:{...item},view:state.workView,...options};
    noticeRenderGuidance(true);
  }
  function noticeRenderGuidance(scroll=false){
    const context=noticeGuidance;
    if(!context||String(state.currentRoom?.chatId)!==context.roomId)return;
    const root=context.root?document.querySelector(context.root):state.modal;
    if(!root?.isConnected)return;
    const explicitTarget=context.target?root.querySelector(context.target):null;
    if(context.target&&!explicitTarget){
      // 대상이 아직 렌더되지 않은 순간에는 안내를 루트/백드롭에 붙이지 않는다.
      // 동적 화면이 다시 그려지면 noticeRenderGuidance()가 재호출되어 올바른 위치에 붙는다.
      root.querySelectorAll(':scope > .rpcm-notice-guidance').forEach(node=>node.remove());
      return;
    }
    const target=explicitTarget||root.querySelector('.rpcm-work-content')||root;
    if(!target)return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-notice-guidance').forEach(node=>{if(node.parentElement!==target)node.remove();});
    for(let parent=target.parentElement;parent&&parent!==root;parent=parent.parentElement)if(parent.tagName==='DETAILS')parent.open=true;
    let guide=target.querySelector(':scope > .rpcm-notice-guidance');
    if(!guide){
      guide=document.createElement('aside');guide.className='rpcm-notice-guidance';guide.tabIndex=-1;guide.setAttribute('aria-label','알림에서 이어서 할 일');
      guide.innerHTML=`<div><small>알림에서 이어서 할 일</small><strong>${esc(context.item.title)}</strong><p>${esc(context.next)}</p>${context.item.message?`<details><summary>알림 내용</summary><p>${esc(context.item.message)}</p></details>`:''}</div><button type="button" data-notice-guide-close aria-label="안내 닫기">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button>${context.choices?.length?`<div class="rpcm-notice-task-choices">${context.choices.map(choice=>`<button type="button" data-notice-guide-task="${esc(choice.task)}"><span>${esc(choice.title)}</span><small>${esc(choice.label)}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`:''}`;
      target.prepend(guide);
      guide.querySelector('[data-notice-guide-close]').onclick=()=>noticeClearGuidance();
      guide.querySelectorAll('[data-notice-guide-task]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{
        if(String(state.currentRoom?.chatId)!==context.roomId)return;
        button.disabled=true;
        try{await noticeOpenDestination(state.currentRoom,{...context.item,targetRef:button.dataset.noticeGuideTask});}
        catch(error){button.disabled=false;guide.querySelector('p').textContent='작업을 열지 못했습니다: '+error.message;}
      });
    }
    root.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-notice-next-action').forEach(node=>node.classList.remove('rpcm-notice-next-action'));
    const action=context.action?target.querySelector(context.action)||root.querySelector(context.action):null;
    if(action&&!action.hidden){action.classList.add('rpcm-notice-next-action');for(let parent=action.parentElement;parent&&parent!==target;parent=parent.parentElement)if(parent.tagName==='DETAILS')parent.open=true;}
    if(scroll){guide.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'auto'});guide.focus({preventScroll:true});}
  }
  async function noticeOpenDestination(room,item){
    if(state.currentRoom!==room)return;
    const epoch=++noticeNavigationEpoch;
    noticeClearGuidance();
    if(!state.modal?.isConnected)await openModal();
    if(state.currentRoom!==room||epoch!==noticeNavigationEpoch)return;
    const current=()=>state.currentRoom===room&&epoch===noticeNavigationEpoch;
    const show=options=>{if(current())noticeShowGuidance(room,item,options);};
    const work=view=>showWorkView(state.modal,view,false);
    const key=String(item.dedupeKey||''),ref=String(item.targetRef||'');
    if(key.startsWith('ai-memory:draft:')&&Object.hasOwn(AI_MEMORY_AREAS,ref)){
      work('tools');
      if(aiMemoryConfig(room).areas[ref]?.draft){
        aiMemoryReviewDialog(room,ref);
        show({root:'#rpcm-ai-memory-backdrop',target:'.rpcm-update-result-body',next:'변경 내용을 확인하고 적용할 항목을 선택한 뒤, 아래의 ‘선택한 항목 적용’을 눌러 주세요. 원문 수정 후에는 ‘변경 내용 확인’을 다시 눌러 주세요.',action:'[data-ai-memory-check]'});
      }else show({target:`[data-ai-memory-area="${CSS.escape(ref)}"]`,next:'이 초안은 이미 처리됐거나 삭제됐습니다. 현재 상태를 확인하고 새 검토가 필요할 때만 ‘바로 실행’을 눌러 주세요.',action:'[data-ai-memory-run]'});
      return;
    }
    if(key==='ai-review:pending'){
      work('tools');const pending=(room.aiReviewInbox||[]).find(row=>row?.status==='pending');
      openAiReviewInboxDialog(room,pending?.jobId||'');
      show({root:'#rpcm-ai-review-backdrop',target:'.rpcm-ai-review-dialog',next:pending?'왼쪽에서 대기 결과를 선택하고 오른쪽 내용을 확인하세요. 적용할 결과만 검토 후 저장하세요.':'대기 중인 결과가 없습니다. 이미 처리한 결과는 기록에서 확인할 수 있습니다.'});return;
    }
    if(key.startsWith('ai-memory:error:')&&Object.hasOwn(AI_MEMORY_AREAS,ref)){
      work('tools');show({target:`[data-ai-memory-area="${CSS.escape(ref)}"]`,next:'아래 실패 내용을 확인하세요. 연결·모델 문제는 API 설정에서 고친 뒤, 이 항목의 ‘바로 실행’으로 다시 시도할 수 있습니다. 다시 실행하면 API를 호출합니다.',action:'[data-ai-memory-run]'});return;
    }
    if(key.startsWith('manual:integrity:')&&Object.hasOwn(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS,ref)){
      work('update');await openManualIntegrityDialog(room);if(!current())return;
      show({root:'#rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop',target:`[data-integrity-task="${CSS.escape(ref)}"]`,next:'이 항목의 현재 저장된 본문을 펼쳐 확인하세요. 직접 반영한 내용이 맞으면 ‘이 수동 편집본 확인’을, 누락된 내용은 보관한 결과로 복구·재적용하세요.',action:'[data-confirm-manual-current]'});return;
    }
    if(key==='longterm:external-packet'||key==='longterm:auto-draft'){
      const meta=longTermState(room),packet=meta.drafts.externalPacket;
      const ready=key==='longterm:external-packet'?packet:meta.firstStage.pendingApply||meta.firstStage.autoDraft;
      work('update');const kind=packet&&key==='longterm:external-packet'?packet.kind:'first';
      if(ready){
        await openLongTermUpdateDialog(room,kind,'result');if(!current())return;
        const bulk=document.querySelector('.rpcm-ltm-bulk-backdrop');
        show(bulk?{root:'.rpcm-ltm-bulk-backdrop',target:'.rpcm-ltm-review-editor',next:'위의 기존 기억과 아래 새 결과를 비교·편집한 뒤 ‘미리보기’를 눌러 주세요. 오른쪽 변경 내용을 확인한 다음에 저장하세요.',action:'[data-ltm-bulk-preview]'}:{root:'#rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop',target:'.rpcm-ltm-update-body',next:'외부 AI 결과 TXT를 선택하거나 붙여넣고 ‘결과 미리보기’를 눌러 주세요. 형식 오류를 고친 뒤 기존 기억과 비교해 저장하세요.',action:'[data-ltm-action="import-preview"]'});
      }else show({target:`[data-ltm-update-card="${kind}"]`,next:'이 작업은 이미 처리됐거나 취소됐습니다. 새로운 결과가 필요할 때 ‘작업자료 만들기’로 시작하세요.',action:'[data-ltm-update-packet]'});
      return;
    }
    if(item.targetRef==='refiner'){
      work('ai');const id=key.match(/^refiner:(?:proposal|error|stale|ambiguous):(.*)$/)?.[1]||'';
      const refiner=window.__WISH_RP_MANAGER_REFINER__;
      if(refiner?.openFromNotice)refiner.openFromNotice(id);
      else refiner?.openSettings();
      show({root:'#wish-rpcore-refiner-modal',target:'.rr-settings-sheet',next:'처리기록에서 이 응답의 교정본·실패 사유를 확인하세요. 교정본은 펼쳐 복사할 수 있습니다. 재검수가 필요하면 대상 응답을 확인한 뒤 직접 실행하세요.'});return;
    }
    if(item.type==='longterm'||key==='update:longterm'){
      work('update');show({target:`[data-ltm-update-card="${item.type==='longterm'?'second':'first'}"]`,next:item.type==='longterm'?'현재 확보된 1차 기억을 압축하려면 이 카드의 ‘작업자료 만들기’를 눌러 주세요. 이미 준비한 결과가 있으면 ‘결과 가져오기’를 사용하세요.':'새 RP를 장기기억으로 정리하려면 이 카드의 ‘작업자료 만들기’를 눌러 범위를 선택하세요.',action:item.type==='longterm'?'[data-ltm-update-packet="second"]':'[data-ltm-update-packet="first"]'});return;
    }
    if(key==='manual:waiting'&&!Object.hasOwn(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS,ref)){
      const jobs=Object.entries(room.manualPendingJobs||{}).filter(([task,job])=>Object.hasOwn(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS,task)&&job&&!['applied','cancelled'].includes(job.status));
      if(jobs.length===1)return noticeOpenDestination(room,{...item,targetRef:jobs[0][0]});
      work('update');show({target:'.rpcm-update-list',next:jobs.length?'이어서 진행할 작업을 아래에서 선택하세요. 해당 작업의 결과 가져오기 창으로 바로 연결합니다.':'대기 중인 외부 작업이 없습니다. 이미 저장했거나 취소된 작업입니다.',choices:jobs.map(([task,job])=>({task,title:MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title,label:job.status==='result-imported'?'가져온 결과 검토':job.status==='reviewed'?'변경 내용 재확인·저장':job.status==='stale'?'원문 확인·재검토':'외부 결과 TXT 가져오기'}))});return;
    }
    if(Object.hasOwn(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS,ref)&&(item.targetArea==='update'||key==='manual:waiting')){
      work('update');manualRestoreDrafts(room);refreshManualUpdateCards(state.modal,room);
      const ready=manualResultReady(room,ref);
      if(key==='manual:waiting'&&ready){
        openManualUpdateResultDialog(room,ref);
        show({root:'#rpcm-update-dialog-backdrop',target:'.rpcm-update-result-body',next:'이 작업의 외부 결과 TXT를 가져오거나 붙여넣으세요. ‘형식 검증 · Diff’로 변경을 확인한 뒤 ‘확인하고 저장’을 누르면 반영됩니다.',action:'[data-manual-input]'});
      }else {
        const progress=manualFullBuildFor(room,ref),blocked=ref==='lore'&&!manualLoreGuideSlot(room).ready;
        show({target:`[data-update-task="${CSS.escape(ref)}"]`,next:blocked?'자료집 지침을 먼저 연결해야 합니다. ‘더보기 → 지침만 복사’의 연결 상태와 설정의 자료집 지침을 확인하세요.':ready?'이미 만든 작업자료가 있습니다. 외부 AI의 결과를 받았다면 ‘결과 가져오기’를 누르고 검증 후 저장하세요. 아직 결과가 없다면 ‘작업자료 보기’에서 자료를 다시 받을 수 있습니다.':progress?'이전 구간 저장이 완료됐습니다. ‘다음 구간 작업자료’를 눌러 이어서 진행하세요.':'이 항목의 ‘작업자료 만들기’를 눌러 RP 범위와 지침 포함 여부를 선택하세요. 외부 AI 결과를 받은 뒤 같은 카드에서 가져오면 됩니다.',action:blocked?'':ready?'[data-update-result]':progress?'[data-full-next]':'[data-update-packet]'});
      }return;
    }
    if(item.type==='error'){
      work('tools');openRpcmIssueLogDialog(room);
      show({root:'#rpcm-issue-log-backdrop',target:'.rpcm-update-result-body',next:'알림의 오류 문구와 아래 처리·오류 기록을 확인하세요. 원인이 불분명하면 해당 기록의 ‘복사’로 내용을 보관할 수 있습니다. 저장 실패가 있었다면 결과 원문을 남기고 해당 작업에서 다시 검증하세요.'});return;
    }
    if(item.type==='stale'&&item.targetArea==='update'){
      const jobs=Object.entries(room.manualPendingJobs||{}).filter(([task,job])=>Object.hasOwn(MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS,task)&&job?.status==='stale');
      work('update');show({target:'.rpcm-update-list',next:'응답 교정으로 작업의 원문이 바뀌었습니다. 아래 작업을 선택해 기존 결과를 재검토하거나 최신 원문으로 작업자료를 다시 만드세요.',choices:jobs.map(([task])=>({task,title:MANUAL_UPDATE_TASKS[task].title,label:'재검토할 작업 열기'}))});return;
    }
    const views={update:'update',longterm:'longterm',tools:'tools',ai:'ai',memory:'memory',timeline:'timeline',people:'people',item:'items',items:'items',lore:'lore'};
    const view=views[item.targetArea]||'overview';work(view);
    const sections={memory:'basic',items:'item'};
    show({target:'#rpcm-section-'+(sections[view]||view),next:'아래 항목을 알림 내용과 대조해 확인하세요. 처리가 끝났으면 알림센터에서 확인 또는 삭제할 수 있습니다.'});
  }

  function openNoticeCenter(room){
    noticeReconcile(room);ensureNoticeStyles();
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-notice-backdrop')?.remove();
    const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-notice-backdrop';
    const close=()=>{backdrop.remove();updateNoticeBadge(room);};
    let tab='active',candidateIds=new Set(),selectedNoticeIds=new Set();
    const render=(resetScroll=false)=>{
      const previous=backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-notice-dialog'),scroll=resetScroll?0:previous?.scrollTop||0;
      const focused=previous?.contains(document.activeElement)?document.activeElement:null;
      const focusAttr=focused?[...focused.attributes].find(attr=>/^data-notice-/.test(attr.name)):null;
      const center=noticeState(room),items=center.items.filter(item=>tab==='active'?!item.resolved&&!noticePausedAiMemoryError(room,item):tab==='archive'?item.resolved:false);
      selectedNoticeIds=new Set([...selectedNoticeIds].filter(id=>items.some(item=>item.id===id&&!item.resolved)));
      const inventory=longTermRuntime.roomId===String(room.chatId)?longTermRuntime.inventory:[];
      const eligible=longTermSecondEligible(room,inventory);
      if(tab==='candidates'){
        const allowed=new Set(eligible.map(longTermId));
        candidateIds=new Set([...candidateIds].filter(id=>allowed.has(id)));
      }
      backdrop.innerHTML=`<section id="rpcm-notice-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="알림 센터"><header><h2>알림 센터 · ${noticeUnreadCount(room)}개 미확인</h2><button data-notice-action="close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></header><div class="rpcm-notice-tabs"><button data-notice-tab="active" aria-selected="${tab==='active'}">확인할 일</button><button data-notice-tab="archive" aria-selected="${tab==='archive'}">완료 기록</button><button data-notice-tab="settings" aria-selected="${tab==='settings'}">알림 설정</button>${tab==='candidates'?'<button data-notice-tab="candidates" aria-selected="true">정리 후보</button>':''}</div>${tab==='active'&&items.length?`<div class="rpcm-notice-selection"><label><input type="checkbox" data-notice-select-all ${selectedNoticeIds.size===items.length?'checked':''}>전체 선택</label><span>${selectedNoticeIds.size}개 선택</span><button type="button" data-notice-action="delete-selected" ${selectedNoticeIds.size?'':'disabled'}>선택 삭제</button><small>알림만 삭제하며 작업 결과는 유지됩니다.</small></div>`:''}${tab==='candidates'?`<p>고정·자동 정리 보호·보호된 기억을 제외했습니다. 최종 검토에서 원본 유지 또는 백업 후 원본 정리를 선택합니다.</p><p>전체 ${inventory.length}개 · 제외 ${inventory.length-eligible.length}개 · 후보 ${eligible.length}개 · 목표 ${center.longTermLimit}개</p>${inventory.length-eligible.length>=center.longTermLimit?`<p class="rpcm-update-warning">고정·자동 정리 보호·보호된 기억만 ${inventory.length-eligible.length}개입니다. 이번 정리로 ${center.longTermLimit}개 이하를 보장할 수 없습니다. 가능한 범위만 정리하거나 고정 목록을 검토해 주세요.</p>`:''}<div>${eligible.map(item=>`<div class="rpcm-notice-row"><label><input type="checkbox" data-notice-candidate="${esc(longTermId(item))}" ${candidateIds.has(longTermId(item))?'checked':''}><strong>${esc(item.title||'제목 없음')}</strong></label><p>${esc(String(item.summary||'').slice(0,160))}</p><div class="rpcm-notice-actions"><button type="button" data-notice-action="pin-candidate" data-notice-memory-id="${esc(longTermId(item))}">고정하고 제외</button><button type="button" data-notice-action="final-candidate" data-notice-memory-id="${esc(longTermId(item))}">자동 정리 보호으로 표시</button></div></div>`).join('')||'<p>정리 가능한 후보가 부족합니다.</p>'}</div><button data-notice-action="generate-second" ${candidateIds.size<2?'disabled':''}>선택한 ${candidateIds.size}개로 2차 AI 초안 만들기</button>`:tab==='settings'?`<div class="rpcm-notice-settings"><label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>알림 사용</span><input type="checkbox" data-notice-setting="enabled" ${center.enabled?'checked':''}></label><label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>새 RP 안내 주기</span><span class="rpcm-pref-controls"><input type="number" min="5" max="1000" data-notice-setting="updateTurns" value="${center.updateTurns}"><small>턴</small></span></label><label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>장기기억 정리 안내</span><span class="rpcm-pref-controls"><input type="number" min="5" max="200" data-notice-setting="longTermLimit" value="${center.longTermLimit}"><small>개부터</small></span></label><fieldset><legend>종류별 알림</legend>${Object.entries(NOTICE_CATEGORIES).map(([key,label])=>`<label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>${esc(label)}</span><input type="checkbox" data-notice-category="${key}" ${center.categories[key]!==false?'checked':''}></label>`).join('')}</fieldset><fieldset><legend>영역별 갱신 안내</legend>${Object.entries(noticeUpdateAreas()).map(([key,info])=>`<label class="rpcm-pref-row"><span>${esc(info.title)}</span><span class="rpcm-pref-controls"><input type="checkbox" data-notice-area="${key}" ${center.areaEnabled[key]!==false?'checked':''}><input type="number" min="5" max="1000" data-notice-area-turns="${key}" value="${Number(center.areaTurns[key])||center.updateTurns}"><small>턴</small></span></label>`).join('')}</fieldset></div>`:items.length?items.map(item=>`<article class="rpcm-notice-row ${item.read?'':'is-unread'}" data-notice-id="${esc(item.id)}">${tab==='active'?`<label class="rpcm-notice-select-row"><input type="checkbox" data-notice-select="${esc(item.id)}" ${selectedNoticeIds.has(item.id)?'checked':''}><strong>${esc(item.title)}</strong></label>`:`<strong>${esc(item.title)}</strong>`}<p>${esc(item.message)}</p><small>${esc(NOTICE_CATEGORIES[item.type]||item.type)} · ${new Date(item.updatedAt||item.createdAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</small><div class="rpcm-notice-actions">${!item.resolved?`<button data-notice-action="go">${esc(noticeActionLabel(item))}</button>${item.type==='longterm'?'<button data-notice-action="candidates">정리 후보 보기</button>':''}<button data-notice-action="read">확인</button><button data-notice-action="snooze">내일 다시 알림</button><button data-notice-action="ignore">이번에는 무시</button><button data-notice-action="delete">삭제</button>`:''}</div></article>`).join(''):'<p>표시할 알림이 없습니다.</p>'}</section>`;
      const dialog=backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-notice-dialog');
      if(!resetScroll&&focusAttr)dialog.querySelector(`[${focusAttr.name}="${CSS.escape(focusAttr.value)}"]`)?.focus({preventScroll:true});
      dialog.scrollTop=scroll;
      const all=dialog.querySelector('[data-notice-select-all]');if(all)all.indeterminate=selectedNoticeIds.size>0&&selectedNoticeIds.size<items.length;
    };
    backdrop.onclick=async event=>{
      if(event.target===backdrop){close();return;}
      const tabButton=event.target.closest('[data-notice-tab]');if(tabButton){tab=tabButton.dataset.noticeTab;render(true);return;}
      const button=event.target.closest('[data-notice-action]');if(!button)return;
      const action=button.dataset.noticeAction;if(action==='close'){close();return;}
      if(state.currentRoom!==room){close();notify('방이 바뀌었습니다. 현재 방의 알림을 다시 열어 주세요.','warn');return;}
      if(action==='delete-selected'||action==='delete'){
        const ids=action==='delete-selected'?[...selectedNoticeIds]:[button.closest('[data-notice-id]')?.dataset.noticeId];
        noticeDismissSelected(room,ids);ids.forEach(id=>selectedNoticeIds.delete(id));render();return;
      }
      if(action==='pin-candidate'||action==='final-candidate'){
        const id=String(button.dataset.noticeMemoryId||'');
        if(!longTermSecondEligible(room,longTermRuntime.inventory).some(row=>longTermId(row)===id)){
          notify('이 기억은 정리 후보가 아닙니다. 목록을 다시 확인해 주세요.','warn');render();return;
        }
        const meta=longTermState(room),field=action==='pin-candidate'?'protectedIds':'finalCompressedIds';
        const before=[...meta[field]];meta[field]=[...new Set([...before,id])];
        try{await saveRoom(room);candidateIds.delete(id);render();noticeReconcile(room);updateNoticeBadge(room);}
        catch(error){meta[field]=before;notify('기억 상태를 저장하지 못했습니다: '+error.message,'error');}
        return;
      }
      if(action==='generate-second'){
        const ids=[...candidateIds];if(ids.length<2)return;
        button.disabled=true;button.textContent='AI 초안 만드는 중…';
        try{const draft=await longTermGenerateSecond(room,ids,{proposal:true});longTermRuntime.draftView='second';close();showWorkView(state.modal,'update',true);await openLongTermUpdateDialog(room,'second','result');notify(`2차 초안 ${draft.cards.length}개를 만들었습니다. 내용을 검토해 주세요.`,'success');}
        catch(error){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='다시 시도';notify(`2차 초안 생성 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');}
        return;
      }
      const item=noticeState(room).items.find(row=>row.id===button.closest('[data-notice-id]')?.dataset.noticeId);if(!item)return;
      if(action==='candidates'){
        if(longTermRuntime.roomId!==String(room.chatId)||!longTermRuntime.inventory.length){
          button.disabled=true;button.textContent='서버 기억 확인 중…';
          try{await longTermRefreshData(room);}catch(error){button.disabled=false;button.textContent='정리 후보 보기';notify(`장기기억 조회 실패: ${error.message}`,'error');return;}
        }
        candidateIds=new Set(longTermSecondEligible(room,longTermRuntime.inventory).map(longTermId));
        item.read=true;item.updatedAt=nowIso();queueRoomAutoSave(room,400);tab='candidates';render();updateNoticeBadge(room);return;
      }
      if(action==='read'||action==='go'){item.read=true;}
      if(action==='ignore'){item.read=true;item.ignoredMessage=item.message;}
      if(action==='snooze'){item.read=true;item.snoozedUntil=Date.now()+86400000;}
      item.updatedAt=nowIso();queueRoomAutoSave(room,400);
      if(action==='go'){
        close();try{await noticeOpenDestination(room,item);}catch(error){
          if(state.currentRoom===room)noticeShowGuidance(room,item,{target:'.rpcm-work-content',next:'이 작업을 열지 못했습니다: '+error.message+' 알림 내용과 해당 작업의 저장 상태를 확인해 주세요.'});
        }
      }else render();updateNoticeBadge(room);
    };
    backdrop.onchange=event=>{
      const center=noticeState(room),target=event.target;
      if(target.hasAttribute('data-notice-select-all')){selectedNoticeIds=target.checked?new Set(center.items.filter(item=>!item.resolved&&!noticePausedAiMemoryError(room,item)).map(item=>item.id)):new Set();render();return;}
      if(target.dataset.noticeSelect){if(target.checked)selectedNoticeIds.add(target.dataset.noticeSelect);else selectedNoticeIds.delete(target.dataset.noticeSelect);render();return;}
      if(target.dataset.noticeCandidate){if(target.checked)candidateIds.add(target.dataset.noticeCandidate);else candidateIds.delete(target.dataset.noticeCandidate);render();return;}
      if(target.dataset.noticeSetting==='enabled')center.enabled=target.checked;
      else if(target.dataset.noticeSetting==='updateTurns')center.updateTurns=Math.max(5,Math.min(1000,Number(target.value)||30));
      else if(target.dataset.noticeSetting==='longTermLimit')center.longTermLimit=Math.max(5,Math.min(200,Number(target.value)||30));
      else if(target.dataset.noticeCategory)center.categories[target.dataset.noticeCategory]=target.checked;
      else if(target.dataset.noticeArea)center.areaEnabled[target.dataset.noticeArea]=target.checked;
      else if(target.dataset.noticeAreaTurns)center.areaTurns[target.dataset.noticeAreaTurns]=Math.max(5,Math.min(1000,Number(target.value)||center.updateTurns));
      else return;
      queueRoomAutoSave(room,400);noticeReconcile(room);render();updateNoticeBadge(room);
    };
    backdrop.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape')close();};render();document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
  }

  // 기타·OOC 설정집은 항목의 category를 그대로 보존하고 폴더를 선택 단위로 다룹니다.

  function extraLibraryFolderGroups(items = [], folderNames = []) {
    const groups = new Map();
    const add = name => {
      const category = String(name || '').trim() || '미분류';
      if (!groups.has(category)) groups.set(category, []);
      return category;
    };
    (Array.isArray(folderNames) ? folderNames : []).forEach(add);
    (Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).forEach((item, index) => {
      const category = add(item?.category ?? item?.extraCategory ?? '');
      groups.get(category).push({ item, index });
    });
    return groups;
  }

  function extraRoomFolderNames(room) {
    return [...extraLibraryFolderGroups((room?.slots || []).filter(slot => slot.group === 'extra').map(slot => ({
      category: String(slot.extraCategory || '').trim(),
    }))).keys()];
  }

  function extraSavedFolderNames(lib) {
    return [...extraLibraryFolderGroups(lib?.extras || [], lib?.folders || []).keys()];
  }

  function extraLibraryPreviewHtml(items = [], folders = []) {
    const groups = extraLibraryFolderGroups(items, folders);
    if (!groups.size) return '<div class="rpcm-extra-folder-empty">저장된 폴더가 없습니다.</div>';
    return [...groups].map(([category, rows]) => `<details class="rpcm-extra-library-folder">
      <summary><span>📁 ${esc(category)}</span><small>${rows.length}개 항목</small></summary>
      <div class="rpcm-extra-library-folder-body">${rows.length ? rows.map(({item}) => `<details class="rpcm-extra-library-card">
        <summary><strong>${esc(String(item.title || '제목 없음'))}</strong><small>${formatCount(String(item.content || '').length)}자</small></summary>
        <pre>${esc(String(item.content || ''))}</pre>
      </details>`).join('') : '<div class="rpcm-extra-folder-empty">비어 있는 폴더입니다.</div>'}</div>
    </details>`).join('');
  }

  function openExtraFolderSelectionDialog({ title, description = '', items = [], folders = [], confirmText = '선택 항목 저장', preserveOption = false, preserveDefault = true }) {
    items = items.map(item => item && typeof item === 'object' ? {...item} : item);
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      document.getElementById('rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop')?.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop';
      const groups = extraLibraryFolderGroups(items, folders);
      if (!groups.size) { notify('선택할 기타 폴더가 없습니다.', 'warn'); resolve(null); return; }
      backdrop.innerHTML = `<div class="rpcm-lib-dialog rpcm-extra-library-select" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${esc(title)}">
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">${esc(title)}</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">${esc(description || '폴더를 체크하면 내부 항목을 모두 선택합니다. 폴더와 항목을 펼쳐 내용도 확인할 수 있어요.')}</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
        <div class="rpcm-lib-toolbar"><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" data-act="all">전체 선택</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" data-act="none">전체 해제</button><span class="rpcm-lib-selected" aria-live="polite"></span></div>
        <div class="rpcm-lib-list rpcm-extra-folder-select-list">${[...groups].map(([category, members], folderIndex) => `<section class="rpcm-extra-choice-folder" data-extra-folder="${folderIndex}">
          <div class="rpcm-extra-choice-head"><label><input type="checkbox" data-extra-folder-check="${folderIndex}" checked><strong>📁 ${esc(category)}</strong><small>${members.length}개</small></label><button type="button" data-extra-folder-toggle="${folderIndex}" aria-expanded="false">펼쳐보기 <span aria-hidden="true">▾</span></button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-extra-choice-body" data-extra-folder-body="${folderIndex}" hidden>${members.length ? members.map(({item,index}) => `<div class="rpcm-extra-choice-item">
            <label><input type="checkbox" data-extra-item-index="${index}" data-extra-parent="${folderIndex}" checked><strong>${esc(String(item.title || '기타 항목'))}</strong><small>${formatCount(String(item.content || '').length)}자</small></label>
            <details><summary>내용 보기</summary><pre>${esc(String(item.content || ''))}</pre></details>
          </div>`).join('') : '<div class="rpcm-extra-folder-empty">내용이 없는 폴더입니다. 폴더 이름만 저장됩니다.</div>'}</div>
        </section>`).join('')}</div>
        ${preserveOption ? `<label class="rpcm-lib-preserve"><input type="checkbox" data-extra-preserve ${preserveDefault ? 'checked' : ''}> 기존 설정집의 미선택 항목·폴더 유지</label>` : ''}
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">${esc(confirmText)}</button></div>
      </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const names = [...groups.keys()];
      const folderBoxes = () => [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-extra-folder-check]')];
      const itemBoxes = () => [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-extra-item-index]')];
      const refresh = () => {
        folderBoxes().forEach(box => {
          const children = itemBoxes().filter(child => child.dataset.extraParent === box.dataset.extraFolderCheck);
          if (children.length) {
            box.checked = children.every(child => child.checked);
            box.indeterminate = children.some(child => child.checked) && !box.checked;
          }
        });
        const foldersSelected = folderBoxes().filter(box => box.checked || box.indeterminate).length;
        const selected = itemBoxes().filter(box => box.checked).length;
        backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-selected').textContent = `${foldersSelected}개 폴더 · ${selected}개 항목 선택`;
      };
      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      folderBoxes().forEach(box => box.onchange = () => {
        const value = box.checked;
        itemBoxes().filter(child => child.dataset.extraParent === box.dataset.extraFolderCheck).forEach(child => { child.checked = value; });
        refresh();
      });
      itemBoxes().forEach(box => box.onchange = refresh);
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-extra-folder-toggle]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
        const body = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-extra-folder-body="${button.dataset.extraFolderToggle}"]`);
        body.hidden = !body.hidden;
        button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!body.hidden));
        button.innerHTML = body.hidden ? '펼쳐보기 <span aria-hidden="true">▾</span>' : '접기 <span aria-hidden="true">▴</span>';
      });
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="all"]').onclick = () => { folderBoxes().forEach(box => { box.checked = true;box.indeterminate=false; });itemBoxes().forEach(box => {box.checked=true;});refresh(); };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="none"]').onclick = () => { folderBoxes().forEach(box => {box.checked=false;box.indeterminate=false;});itemBoxes().forEach(box => {box.checked=false;});refresh(); };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const checked = itemBoxes().filter(box => box.checked);
        const indexes = checked.map(box => Number(box.dataset.extraItemIndex));
        if (new Set(indexes).size !== checked.length || indexes.some(index => !Number.isInteger(index) || !items[index] || typeof items[index] !== 'object')) {
          notify('선택한 기타 항목을 모두 확인하지 못했습니다. 창을 다시 열어 선택해 주세요.', 'error', 6000);return;
        }
        const selected = indexes.map(index => ({...items[index]}));
        const selectedFolders = folderBoxes().filter(box => box.checked || box.indeterminate).map(box => names[Number(box.dataset.extraFolderCheck)]);
        if (!selectedFolders.length) { notify('저장하거나 불러올 폴더를 선택해 주세요.', 'warn');return; }
        finish({items:selected, selectedCount:checked.length, folders:selectedFolders, preserve:preserveOption ? !!backdrop.querySelector('[data-extra-preserve]')?.checked : false});
      };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(null);
      backdrop.onclick = event => { if(event.target === backdrop) finish(null); };
      backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if(event.key === 'Escape') finish(null); };
      refresh();
    });
  }

  function openCharacterSelectionDialog({ title, description = '', items = [], confirmText = '확인', preserveOption = false, preserveDefault = true, itemFallback = '캐릭터', preserveText = '설정집에 이미 있는 미선택 캐릭터는 그대로 유지' }) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const old = document.getElementById('rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop');
      if (old) old.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop';
      const safeItems = items.map((item, index) => ({ item, index }));
      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">${esc(title)}</div>${description ? `<div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">${esc(description)}</div>` : ''}</div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-toolbar"><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" data-act="all">전체 선택</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" data-act="none">전체 해제</button><span class="rpcm-lib-selected">${safeItems.length}개 선택</span></div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-list">${safeItems.map(({item,index}) => `<label class="rpcm-lib-row"><input type="checkbox" data-lib-index="${index}" checked><span><strong>${esc(item.title || itemFallback)}</strong><small>${formatCount(String(item.content || '').length)}자 · ${retentionLabel(item.retentionTurns)}</small></span></label>`).join('')}</div>
          ${preserveOption ? `<label class="rpcm-lib-preserve"><input type="checkbox" id="rpcm-lib-preserve" ${preserveDefault ? 'checked' : ''}><span>${esc(preserveText)}</span></label>` : ''}
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">${esc(confirmText)}</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const selectedEl = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-selected');
      const cbs = () => [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-lib-index]')];
      const refresh = () => { if (selectedEl) selectedEl.textContent = `${cbs().filter(cb => cb.checked).length}개 선택`; };
      cbs().forEach(cb => cb.onchange = refresh);
      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => finish(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="all"]').onclick = () => { cbs().forEach(cb => cb.checked = true); refresh(); };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="none"]').onclick = () => { cbs().forEach(cb => cb.checked = false); refresh(); };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const indexes = cbs().filter(cb => cb.checked).map(cb => Number(cb.dataset.libIndex));
        if (!indexes.length) { notify('하나 이상 선택해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
        const preserve = preserveOption ? !!backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-lib-preserve')?.checked : false;
        finish({ items: indexes.map(i => items[i]).filter(Boolean), preserve });
      };
      backdrop.onclick = e => { if (e.target === backdrop) finish(null); };
    });
  }

  function openStoredLibraryManagerDialog(library, { mode = 'character', allLibraries = [] } = {}) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      document.querySelector('#rpcm-library-manager-backdrop')?.remove();
      const isExtra = mode === 'extra';
      if (isExtra) ensureExtraLibraryItemIds(library);
      const field = isExtra ? 'extras' : 'characters';
      const displayNameOf = lib => isExtra ? extraLibraryDisplayName(lib) : libraryDisplayName(lib);
      const draft = (Array.isArray(library?.[field]) ? library[field] : []).map(item => ({
        ...item,
        aliases: Array.isArray(item?.aliases) ? [...item.aliases] : [],
        content: String(item?.content || ''),
        retentionTurns: normalizeRetentionTurns(item?.retentionTurns),
      }));
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-library-manager-backdrop';
      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog rpcm-library-manager" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${isExtra ? '기타' : '캐릭터'} 설정집 관리">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">${isExtra ? '기타' : '캐릭터'} 설정집 관리</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">설정집 이름과 내부 항목을 직접 수정하거나 필요한 항목만 삭제할 수 있습니다.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-library-name-row"><label>설정집 이름<input type="text" class="rpcm-library-name-input" value="${esc(displayNameOf(library))}" placeholder="설정집 이름"></label><span class="rpcm-library-item-count"></span></div>
          <div class="rpcm-library-manager-toolbar"><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" data-act="select-all">전체 선택</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" data-act="select-none">선택 해제</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small rpcm-library-delete-selected" data-act="delete-selected">선택 삭제</button><span class="rpcm-library-selected-count">0개 선택</span></div>
          <div class="rpcm-library-manager-list"></div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions rpcm-library-manager-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn danger" data-act="delete-library">설정집 전체 삭제</button><span class="rpcm-library-manager-spacer"></span><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="save">변경 저장</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const list = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-library-manager-list');
      const countEl = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-library-item-count');
      const nameInput = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-library-name-input');
      const selectedCountEl = backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-library-selected-count');
      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      const selectedBoxes = () => [...list.querySelectorAll('[data-manager-select]')];
      const refreshSelectedCount = () => { selectedCountEl.textContent = `${selectedBoxes().filter(box => box.checked).length}개 선택`; };

      const renderItems = () => {
        countEl.textContent = `${draft.length}${isExtra ? '개' : '명'}`;
        if (!draft.length) {
          list.innerHTML = '<div class="rpcm-empty">설정집에 남은 항목이 없습니다. 전체 삭제하거나 취소해 주세요.</div>';
          return;
        }
        const renderCard = (item,index) => `
          <details class="rpcm-library-item-card" data-manager-item="${index}">
            <summary><input type="checkbox" data-manager-select="${index}" aria-label="${esc(String(item.title || (isExtra ? '기타' : '캐릭터')))} 선택"><span class="rpcm-library-item-number">${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}.</span><strong data-manager-summary-title>${esc(String(item.title || (isExtra ? '기타' : '캐릭터')))}</strong><span>${formatCount(String(item.content || '').length)}자 · ${retentionLabel(item.retentionTurns)}</span><button type="button" class="rpcm-library-item-delete" data-manager-delete="${index}">삭제</button><span class="rpcm-chevron">▶</span></summary>
            <div class="rpcm-library-item-edit">
              <label>항목 이름<input type="text" data-manager-title value="${esc(String(item.title || ''))}" placeholder="${isExtra ? '기타 항목 이름' : '캐릭터 이름'}"></label>
              ${isExtra ? `<label>폴더(카테고리)<input type="text" data-manager-category value="${esc(String(item.category ?? item.extraCategory ?? ''))}" placeholder="예: 성인지침 · 문체"></label>` : `<label>자동 선택용 별칭 · 주입 안 됨<input type="text" data-manager-aliases value="${esc((item.aliases || []).join(', '))}" placeholder="애칭·약칭·호칭을 쉼표로 구분"></label>`}
              <label class="rpcm-library-retention-label">유지 주기<select data-manager-retention>${retentionOptionsHtml(item.retentionTurns)}</select></label>
              <label>내용<textarea data-manager-content data-rpcm-editor="true" spellcheck="false">${esc(String(item.content || ''))}</textarea></label>
            </div>
          </details>`;
        list.innerHTML = isExtra ? [...extraLibraryFolderGroups(draft, library.folders || [])].map(([category,members]) => `<details class="rpcm-extra-manager-folder" open><summary>📁 ${esc(category)} <small>${members.length}개</small></summary><div>${members.map(({item,index})=>renderCard(item,index)).join('')}</div></details>`).join('') : draft.map(renderCard).join('');

        list.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-library-item-card').forEach(card => {
          const index = Number(card.dataset.managerItem);
          const item = draft[index];
          const titleInput = card.querySelector('[data-manager-title]');
          const aliasInput = card.querySelector('[data-manager-aliases]');
          const categoryInput = card.querySelector('[data-manager-category]');
          const retentionInput = card.querySelector('[data-manager-retention]');
          const contentInput = card.querySelector('[data-manager-content]');
          const summaryTitle = card.querySelector('[data-manager-summary-title]');
          titleInput.oninput = () => { item.title = titleInput.value; summaryTitle.textContent = titleInput.value || (isExtra ? '기타' : '캐릭터'); };
          if (aliasInput) aliasInput.oninput = () => { item.aliases = aliasInput.value.split(',').map(value => value.trim()).filter(Boolean); };
          if (categoryInput) { categoryInput.oninput = () => { item.category = categoryInput.value.trim(); }; categoryInput.onchange = () => renderItems(); }
          retentionInput.onchange = () => { item.retentionTurns = normalizeRetentionTurns(retentionInput.value); };
          contentInput.oninput = () => { item.content = contentInput.value; };
          card.addEventListener('toggle', () => { card.querySelector('.rpcm-chevron').textContent = card.open ? '▼' : '▶'; });
        });
        selectedBoxes().forEach(box => {
          box.onclick = event => event.stopPropagation();
          box.onchange = refreshSelectedCount;
        });
        refreshSelectedCount();
        list.querySelectorAll('[data-manager-delete]').forEach(button => {
          button.onclick = event => {
            event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
            const index = Number(button.dataset.managerDelete);
            const title = String(draft[index]?.title || (isExtra ? '기타' : '캐릭터'));
            if (!confirm(`설정집에서 ‘${title}’ 항목을 삭제할까요?\n‘변경 저장’을 눌러야 최종 반영됩니다.`)) return;
            draft.splice(index, 1);
            renderItems();
          };
        });
      };

      renderItems();
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="select-all"]').onclick = () => { selectedBoxes().forEach(box => { box.checked = true; }); refreshSelectedCount(); };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="select-none"]').onclick = () => { selectedBoxes().forEach(box => { box.checked = false; }); refreshSelectedCount(); };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="delete-selected"]').onclick = () => {
        const indexes = selectedBoxes().filter(box => box.checked).map(box => Number(box.dataset.managerSelect)).sort((a,b) => b - a);
        if (!indexes.length) { notify('삭제할 항목을 먼저 선택해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
        if (!confirm(`선택한 ${indexes.length}개 항목을 설정집에서 삭제할까요?\n‘변경 저장’을 눌러야 최종 반영됩니다.`)) return;
        indexes.forEach(index => draft.splice(index, 1));
        renderItems();
      };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => finish(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="save"]').onclick = async () => {
        const nextName = String(nameInput.value || '').trim();
        if (!nextName) { notify('설정집 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn'); nameInput.focus(); return; }
        const duplicatedLibraryName = (allLibraries || []).some(other => other && String(other.scopeId || '') !== String(library.scopeId || '') && normalizedLibraryLabel(displayNameOf(other)) === normalizedLibraryLabel(nextName));
        if (duplicatedLibraryName) { notify('같은 이름의 설정집이 이미 있습니다.', 'warn', 4500); nameInput.focus(); return; }
        if (!draft.length) { notify('항목이 하나도 없습니다. 설정집 전체 삭제를 이용해 주세요.', 'warn', 4500); return; }
        const titleKeys = draft.map(item => normalizedLibraryLabel(item.title));
        if (titleKeys.some(key => !key)) { notify('모든 항목의 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
        if (!isExtra && new Set(titleKeys).size !== titleKeys.length) { notify('설정집 안에 같은 이름의 캐릭터가 두 명 있습니다. 이름을 다르게 수정해 주세요.', 'warn', 5200); return; }
        try {
          const updated = {
            ...library,
            presetName: nextName,
            label: nextName,
            [field]: draft.map(item => ({
              ...item,
              title: String(item.title || '').trim(),
              content: String(item.content || ''),
              retentionTurns: normalizeRetentionTurns(item.retentionTurns),
              ...(isExtra ? {category:String(item.category || '').trim()} : { aliases:[...new Set((item.aliases || []).map(value => String(value).trim()).filter(Boolean))] }),
            })),
            // 기존부터 비어 있던 폴더만 이름을 보존합니다. 항목을 다른 폴더로 옮겼다면 유령 폴더를 만들지 않습니다.
            ...(isExtra ? {folders:[...new Set([...(library.folders || []).filter(name => !(library.extras || []).some(item => (String(item.category || '').trim() || '미분류') === name)),...extraLibraryFolderGroups(draft).keys()])]} : {}),
          };
          await saveCharacterLibrary(updated);
          notify(`‘${nextName}’ 설정집 변경을 저장했습니다.`, 'success', 4200);
          finish({ action:'saved', library:updated });
        } catch (error) { notify(`설정집 변경 저장 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
      };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="delete-library"]').onclick = async () => {
        const name = displayNameOf(library);
        if (!confirm(`‘${name}’ 설정집 전체를 삭제할까요?\n내부 항목 ${draft.length}${isExtra ? '개' : '명'}도 함께 삭제되며 되돌릴 수 없습니다.`)) return;
        try {
          await deleteStoredLibrary(library.scopeId);
          notify(`‘${name}’ 설정집을 삭제했습니다.`, 'success', 4200);
          finish({ action:'deleted', scopeId:library.scopeId });
        } catch (error) { notify(`설정집 삭제 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
      };
      backdrop.onclick = event => { if (event.target === backdrop) finish(null); };
      backdrop.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') finish(null); };
      if (!isMobileManagerLayout()) setTimeout(() => nameInput.focus(), 0);
    });
  }

  function openLibraryPickerDialog(libraries, { title = '불러올 설정집 선택', confirmText = '이 설정집 선택', mode = 'character' } = {}) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const old = document.getElementById('rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop');
      if (old) old.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-lib-dialog-backdrop';
      const safe = (libraries || []).map((lib, index) => ({ lib, index }));
      const isExtra = mode === 'extra';
      const displayNameOf = lib => isExtra ? extraLibraryDisplayName(lib) : libraryDisplayName(lib);
      const itemCountOf = lib => isExtra ? (Array.isArray(lib?.extras) ? lib.extras.length : 0) : (Array.isArray(lib?.characters) ? lib.characters.length : 0);
      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">${esc(title)}</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">설정집을 선택하거나 ‘항목 관리’를 눌러 내부 내용을 수정·삭제할 수 있습니다.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-list">${safe.map(({lib,index}) => {
            const nm = displayNameOf(lib);
            const count = itemCountOf(lib);
            const source = lib.sourceLabel || lib.sourceLabels?.[0] || '';
            return `<div class="rpcm-lib-row rpcm-library-row ${isExtra ? 'rpcm-extra-library-picker-row' : ''}" data-library-row="${index}"><label class="rpcm-lib-row-main"><input type="radio" name="rpcm-library-choice" data-lib-index="${index}" ${index === 0 ? 'checked' : ''}><span><strong data-lib-name="${index}">${esc(nm)}</strong><small><span data-lib-count="${index}">${count}${isExtra ? '개' : '명'}</span>${source ? ` · 저장 출처 ${esc(source)}` : ''}</small></span></label><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-manage-btn" data-manage-index="${index}">항목 관리</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-rename-icon" data-rename-index="${index}" title="설정집 이름 수정" aria-label="${esc(nm)} 이름 수정"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 20h9"/><path d="M16.5 3.5a2.1 2.1 0 0 1 3 3L7 19l-4 1 1-4Z"/></svg></button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-delete-icon" data-delete-library-index="${index}" title="설정집 전체 삭제" aria-label="${esc(nm)} 설정집 삭제"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 6h18"/><path d="M8 6V4h8v2"/><path d="M19 6l-1 14H6L5 6"/><path d="M10 11v5M14 11v5"/></svg></button>${isExtra ? `<details class="rpcm-extra-picker-preview"><summary>저장된 폴더 · ${extraSavedFolderNames(lib).length}개 보기</summary><div>${extraLibraryPreviewHtml(lib.extras,lib.folders)}</div></details>` : ''}</div>`;
          }).join('')}</div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">${esc(confirmText)}</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);
      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => finish(null);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(null);
      const refreshPickerRow = (index, lib) => {
        const nameEl = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-lib-name="${index}"]`);
        const countEl = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-lib-count="${index}"]`);
        const renameBtn = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-rename-index="${index}"]`);
        const deleteBtn = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-delete-library-index="${index}"]`);
        const displayName = displayNameOf(lib);
        if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = displayNameOf(lib);
        if (countEl) countEl.textContent = `${itemCountOf(lib)}${isExtra ? '개' : '명'}`;
        if (renameBtn) renameBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${displayName} 이름 수정`);
        if (deleteBtn) deleteBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${displayName} 설정집 삭제`);
        // 항목 관리에서 바뀐 폴더·원문을 설정집 선택창에도 즉시 갱신합니다.
        if (isExtra) {
          const preview = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-library-row="${index}"] .rpcm-extra-picker-preview`);
          if (preview) {
            const wasOpen = preview.open;
            preview.innerHTML = `<summary>저장된 폴더 · ${extraSavedFolderNames(lib).length}개 보기</summary><div>${extraLibraryPreviewHtml(lib.extras,lib.folders)}</div>`;
            preview.open = wasOpen;
          }
        }
      };
      const removePickerRow = index => {
        libraries[index] = null;
        backdrop.querySelector(`[data-library-row="${index}"]`)?.remove();
        const firstRadio = backdrop.querySelector('input[name="rpcm-library-choice"]');
        if (firstRadio) firstRadio.checked = true;
        else finish(null);
      };
      async function renameLibraryAtIndex(index) {
        const lib = libraries[index];
        if (!lib) return;
        const before = displayNameOf(lib);
        const entered = prompt('설정집 새 이름을 입력해 주세요.', before);
        if (entered === null) return;
        const nextName = String(entered || '').trim();
        if (!nextName) { notify('설정집 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
        if (nextName === before) return;
        const nextKey = normalizedLibraryLabel(nextName);
        const duplicated = libraries.some((other, otherIndex) => other && otherIndex !== index && normalizedLibraryLabel(displayNameOf(other)) === nextKey);
        if (duplicated) { notify('같은 이름의 설정집이 이미 있습니다.', 'warn', 4500); return; }
        try {
          const updated = { ...lib, presetName: nextName, label: nextName };
          await saveCharacterLibrary(updated);
          libraries[index] = updated;
          refreshPickerRow(index, updated);
          const renameBtn = backdrop.querySelector(`[data-rename-index="${index}"]`);
          if (renameBtn) {
            renameBtn.title = '설정집 이름 수정';
            renameBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', `${nextName} 이름 수정`);
          }
          if (!isExtra) {
            const autoOpt = document.querySelector(`#rpcm-auto-char-library option[value="${CSS.escape(String(updated.scopeId || ''))}"]`);
            if (autoOpt) autoOpt.textContent = `${nextName} (${Array.isArray(updated.characters) ? updated.characters.length : 0}명)`;
          }
          notify(`설정집 이름을 ‘${nextName}’(으)로 수정했습니다.`, 'success', 4200);
        } catch (e) {
          notify(`설정집 이름 수정 실패: ${e.message}`, 'error', 5500);
        }
      }
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-rename-index]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.onclick = async e => {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          const index = Number(btn.dataset.renameIndex);
          const radio = backdrop.querySelector(`input[name="rpcm-library-choice"][data-lib-index="${index}"]`);
          if (radio) radio.checked = true;
          await renameLibraryAtIndex(index);
        };
      });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-manage-index]').forEach(button => {
        button.onclick = async event => {
          event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
          const index = Number(button.dataset.manageIndex);
          const library = libraries[index];
          if (!library) return;
          const radio = backdrop.querySelector(`input[name="rpcm-library-choice"][data-lib-index="${index}"]`);
          if (radio) radio.checked = true;
          const result = await openStoredLibraryManagerDialog(library, { mode, allLibraries:libraries.filter(Boolean) });
          if (!result) return;
          if (result.action === 'deleted') { removePickerRow(index); return; }
          if (result.action === 'saved' && result.library) {
            libraries[index] = result.library;
            refreshPickerRow(index, result.library);
            if (!isExtra) {
              const autoOpt = document.querySelector(`#rpcm-auto-char-library option[value="${CSS.escape(String(result.library.scopeId || ''))}"]`);
              if (autoOpt) autoOpt.textContent = `${libraryDisplayName(result.library)} (${itemCountOf(result.library)}명)`;
            }
          }
        };
      });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('[data-delete-library-index]').forEach(button => {
        button.onclick = async event => {
          event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation();
          const index = Number(button.dataset.deleteLibraryIndex);
          const library = libraries[index];
          if (!library) return;
          const name = displayNameOf(library);
          if (!confirm(`‘${name}’ 설정집 전체를 삭제할까요?\n내부 항목 ${itemCountOf(library)}${isExtra ? '개' : '명'}도 함께 삭제되며 되돌릴 수 없습니다.`)) return;
          try {
            await deleteStoredLibrary(library.scopeId);
            notify(`‘${name}’ 설정집을 삭제했습니다.`, 'success', 4200);
            removePickerRow(index);
          } catch (error) { notify(`설정집 삭제 실패: ${error.message}`, 'error', 6000); }
        };
      });
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const checked = backdrop.querySelector('input[name="rpcm-library-choice"]:checked');
        if (!checked) { notify('설정집을 하나 선택해 주세요.', 'warn'); return; }
        finish(libraries[Number(checked.dataset.libIndex)] || null);
      };
      backdrop.onclick = e => { if (e.target === backdrop) finish(null); };
    });
  }

  function downloadText(text, filename, mime = 'application/json') {
    const blob = new Blob([text], { type: `${mime};charset=utf-8` });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
  }

  function daysInLogMonth(year, month) {
    const normalizedYear = Number.isInteger(Number(year)) && Number(year) > 0 ? Number(year) : 2000;
    const normalizedMonth = Number(month);
    if (normalizedMonth === 2) {
      const leap = normalizedYear % 4 === 0 && (normalizedYear % 100 !== 0 || normalizedYear % 400 === 0);
      return leap ? 29 : 28;
    }
    return [4, 6, 9, 11].includes(normalizedMonth) ? 30 : 31;
  }

  function openLogDateNormalizerDialog(room) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const log = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
      const originalText = String(log?.content || '');
      const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(originalText);
      if (!blocks.length) {
        notify('수정할 날짜 로그 블록을 찾지 못했습니다.', 'warn', 4200);
        resolve(false);
        return;
      }

      const dated = blocks.filter(b => !b.isUnknown && !b.isSpecialDate);
      const special = blocks.filter(b => b.isSpecialDate);
      const unknown = blocks.filter(b => b.isUnknown);
      const acceptedUnknown = new Set((room.acceptedUnknownLogKeys || []).map(String));
      const old = document.getElementById('rpcm-log-dialog-backdrop');
      if (old) old.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-log-dialog-backdrop';

      const datedHtml = dated.length ? `
        <details class="rpcm-log-year" open>
          <summary><strong>날짜가 있는 로그</strong><span>${dated.length}개</span></summary>
          <div class="rpcm-log-help">이미 정리한 날짜도 언제든 다시 수정할 수 있습니다. 여러 항목을 체크한 뒤 연·월·일 중 필요한 값만 한꺼번에 바꿀 수 있습니다. 일괄 수정에서 비워 둔 값은 기존 날짜를 유지합니다.</div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-groupbar rpcm-date-bulkbar">
            <button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-date-select-all">전체 선택</button>
            <button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-date-select-none">전체 해제</button>
            <strong id="rpcm-date-selected-count">선택 0개</strong>
            <span class="rpcm-date-bulk-fields">
              <label>연 <input id="rpcm-date-bulk-year" type="number" min="1" max="999999" step="1" placeholder="유지"></label>
              <label>월 <input id="rpcm-date-bulk-month" type="number" min="1" max="12" step="1" placeholder="유지"></label>
              <label>일 <input id="rpcm-date-bulk-day" type="number" min="1" max="31" step="1" placeholder="유지"></label>
            </span>
            <button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-date-apply-bulk">선택 날짜 일괄 적용</button>
            <button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-date-clear-year">선택 연도 비우기</button>
          </div>
          ${dated.map(b => `<div class="rpcm-log-row rpcm-date-row" data-log-index="${b.index}"><div class="rpcm-log-row-head"><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;flex:1;min-width:0"><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-date-select"><strong>${esc(b.titleText)}</strong></label><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;flex-wrap:nowrap"><input class="rpcm-date-year" type="number" min="1" max="999999" step="1" value="${b.year || ''}" placeholder="연도" style="width:86px" title="비우면 연도 없는 날짜로 변경"><span style="font-size:10px;color:#777">년</span><input class="rpcm-date-month" type="number" min="1" max="12" step="1" value="${b.month}" style="width:48px"><span style="font-size:10px;color:#777">월</span><input class="rpcm-date-day" type="number" min="1" max="31" step="1" value="${b.day}" style="width:48px"><span style="font-size:10px;color:#777">일</span></div></div><div class="rpcm-log-row-reason">원문: ${esc(b.heading)}</div></div>`).join('')}
        </details>` : '';

      const unknownHtml = unknown.length ? `
        <details class="rpcm-log-year" open>
          <summary><strong>날짜 미상</strong><span>${unknown.length}개</span></summary>
          <div class="rpcm-log-help">날짜를 모르는 사건은 가장 과거의 ‘날짜 미상’ 구역에 그대로 보관합니다. <b>날짜 미상으로 계속 유지</b>를 체크하면 메인 화면의 노란 안내가 사라집니다. 각 항목의 제목과 본문을 직접 고치거나 삭제할 수도 있습니다.</div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-groupbar"><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-unknown-accept-all">모두 날짜 미상으로 유지</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-unknown-accept-none">유지 확인 모두 해제</button></div>
          ${unknown.map(b => `<div class="rpcm-log-row rpcm-date-unknown-row" data-log-index="${b.index}"><div class="rpcm-log-row-head"><strong>${esc(b.titleText)}</strong><input class="rpcm-date-full" type="date" title="실제 날짜를 알게 된 경우에만 지정" style="width:145px"></div><div class="rpcm-unknown-controls"><label><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-unknown-accepted" ${acceptedUnknown.has(String(b.key)) ? 'checked' : ''}> 날짜 미상으로 계속 유지</label><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small rpcm-unknown-toggle">내용 보기·수정</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small rpcm-unknown-delete">삭제</button></div><div class="rpcm-unknown-editor" hidden><label><span>제목·키워드</span><input type="text" class="rpcm-unknown-events" value="${esc(b.events)}" placeholder="사건명·키워드" spellcheck="false"></label><label><span>본문</span><textarea class="rpcm-unknown-body" spellcheck="false">${esc(b.body)}</textarea></label></div><div class="rpcm-log-row-reason">원문: ${esc(b.heading)} · ${formatCount(b.body.length)}자</div></div>`).join('')}
        </details>` : '';

      const specialHtml = special.length ? `
        <details class="rpcm-log-year" open>
          <summary><strong>작품 고유 연호</strong><span>${special.length}개</span></summary>
          <div class="rpcm-log-help">BC·BCE·AD·CE·기원전·서기 표기는 날짜 블록으로 정상 인식됩니다. 일반 연도로 바꾸지 않고 원문 그대로 유지합니다.</div>
          ${special.map(b => `<div class="rpcm-log-row"><div class="rpcm-log-row-head"><strong>${esc(b.titleText)}</strong></div><div class="rpcm-log-row-reason">원문 유지: ${esc(b.heading)}</div></div>`).join('')}
        </details>` : '';

      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-log-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">날짜 / 연도 수정</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">연도 누락 보정뿐 아니라 이미 정리한 날짜도 언제든 다시 수정합니다. 로그 본문은 건드리지 않고 [날짜-사건명] 제목만 변경합니다.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-list">${datedHtml}${specialHtml}${unknownHtml}</div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><div class="rpcm-spacer"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">날짜 수정 적용</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);

      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => finish(false);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(false);
      backdrop.onclick = e => { if (e.target === backdrop) finish(false); };

      const allBtn = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-select-all');
      const noneBtn = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-select-none');
      const applyBulkBtn = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-apply-bulk');
      const clearYearBtn = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-clear-year');
      const selectedDateRows = () => [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-row')].filter(row => row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-select')?.checked);
      const updateSelectedDateCount = () => {
        const selected = selectedDateRows();
        const count = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-selected-count');
        if (count) count.textContent = `선택 ${selected.length}개`;
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-row').forEach(row => row.classList.toggle('is-selected', !!row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-select')?.checked));
      };
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-select').forEach(cb => cb.addEventListener('change', updateSelectedDateCount));
      if (allBtn) allBtn.onclick = () => { backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-select').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = true; }); updateSelectedDateCount(); };
      if (noneBtn) noneBtn.onclick = () => { backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-select').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = false; }); updateSelectedDateCount(); };
      if (applyBulkBtn) applyBulkBtn.onclick = () => {
        const selected = selectedDateRows();
        if (!selected.length) { notify('일괄 수정할 날짜를 먼저 선택해 주세요.', 'warn', 3800); return; }
        const values = {
          year:String(backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-bulk-year')?.value || '').trim(),
          month:String(backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-bulk-month')?.value || '').trim(),
          day:String(backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-date-bulk-day')?.value || '').trim(),
        };
        if (!values.year && !values.month && !values.day) { notify('바꿀 연·월·일 중 하나 이상을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3800); return; }
        const parsed = {
          year:values.year ? Number(values.year) : null,
          month:values.month ? Number(values.month) : null,
          day:values.day ? Number(values.day) : null,
        };
        if (values.year && (!Number.isInteger(parsed.year) || parsed.year < 1 || parsed.year > 999999)) { notify('연도는 1~6자리 숫자로 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3800); return; }
        if (values.month && (!Number.isInteger(parsed.month) || parsed.month < 1 || parsed.month > 12)) { notify('월은 1~12 사이로 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3800); return; }
        if (values.day && (!Number.isInteger(parsed.day) || parsed.day < 1 || parsed.day > 31)) { notify('일은 1~31 사이로 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3800); return; }
        const changes = [];
        for (const row of selected) {
          const currentYearText = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-year')?.value || '').trim();
          const year = values.year ? parsed.year : (currentYearText ? Number(currentYearText) : null);
          const month = values.month ? parsed.month : Number(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-month')?.value || 0);
          const day = values.day ? parsed.day : Number(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-day')?.value || 0);
          const maxDay = daysInLogMonth(year, month);
          if (!Number.isInteger(month) || month < 1 || month > 12 || !Number.isInteger(day) || day < 1 || day > maxDay) {
            const title = row.querySelector('strong')?.textContent?.trim() || '선택한 날짜';
            notify(`${title}: ${year ? `${year}년 ` : ''}${month}월 ${day}일은 올바른 날짜가 아닙니다.`, 'warn', 5200);
            return;
          }
          changes.push({ row, year, month, day });
        }
        changes.forEach(({ row, year, month, day }) => {
          if (values.year) row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-year').value = String(year);
          if (values.month) row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-month').value = String(month);
          if (values.day) row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-day').value = String(day);
        });
        notify(`선택한 날짜 ${changes.length}개의 입력값을 일괄 수정했습니다. 아래 ‘날짜 수정 적용’을 눌러 저장해 주세요.`, 'success', 4200);
      };
      if (clearYearBtn) clearYearBtn.onclick = () => {
        const selected = selectedDateRows();
        if (!selected.length) { notify('연도를 비울 날짜를 먼저 선택해 주세요.', 'warn', 3800); return; }
        selected.forEach(row => { const input = row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-year'); if (input) input.value = ''; });
        notify(`선택한 날짜 ${selected.length}개의 연도를 비웠습니다. 아래 ‘날짜 수정 적용’을 눌러 저장해 주세요.`, 'success', 4200);
      };
      updateSelectedDateCount();
      const acceptAllUnknownBtn = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-unknown-accept-all');
      const clearAcceptedUnknownBtn = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-unknown-accept-none');
      if (acceptAllUnknownBtn) acceptAllUnknownBtn.onclick = () => backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-unknown-accepted').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = true; });
      if (clearAcceptedUnknownBtn) clearAcceptedUnknownBtn.onclick = () => backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-unknown-accepted').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = false; });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-unknown-toggle').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
        const editor = button.closest('.rpcm-date-unknown-row')?.querySelector('.rpcm-unknown-editor');
        if (!editor) return;
        editor.hidden = !editor.hidden;
        button.textContent = editor.hidden ? '내용 보기·수정' : '내용 닫기';
      });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-unknown-delete').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
        const row = button.closest('.rpcm-date-unknown-row');
        if (!row) return;
        const block = blocks[Number(row.dataset.logIndex)];
        const deleting = !row.classList.contains('is-delete-pending');
        if (deleting && !confirm(`‘${block?.titleText || '날짜 미상 로그'}’ 항목을 삭제할까요?\n아래 ‘적용’을 누르기 전에는 저장되지 않습니다.`)) return;
        row.classList.toggle('is-delete-pending', deleting);
        button.textContent = deleting ? '삭제 취소' : '삭제';
        row.querySelectorAll('input,textarea').forEach(input => { input.disabled = deleting; });
      });

      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const replacements = [];
        let invalidMessage = '';

        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-row').forEach(row => {
          if (invalidMessage) return;
          const idx = Number(row.dataset.logIndex);
          const block = blocks[idx];
          if (!block) return;
          const rawYear = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-year')?.value || '').trim();
          const rawMonth = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-month')?.value || '').trim();
          const rawDay = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-day')?.value || '').trim();
          const year = rawYear ? Number(rawYear) : null;
          const month = Number(rawMonth);
          const day = Number(rawDay);
          if (year != null && (!Number.isInteger(year) || year < 1 || year > 999999)) { invalidMessage = `${block.titleText}: 연도를 1~6자리 숫자로 입력해 주세요.`; return; }
          if (!Number.isInteger(month) || month < 1 || month > 12) { invalidMessage = `${block.titleText}: 월은 1~12 사이여야 합니다.`; return; }
          const maxDay = daysInLogMonth(year, month);
          if (!Number.isInteger(day) || day < 1 || day > maxDay) { invalidMessage = `${block.titleText}: ${month}월의 날짜가 올바르지 않습니다.`; return; }
          const changed = (year || null) !== (block.year || null) || month !== block.month || day !== block.day;
          if (!changed) return;
          replacements.push({ start:block.sourceStart, end:block.headingEnd, text:formatNormalizedLogHeading(block, year, month, day) });
        });

        if (invalidMessage) { notify(invalidMessage, 'warn', 5200); return; }

        const deletedIndexes = new Set();
        const desiredAcceptedOldKeys = new Set();
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-date-unknown-row').forEach(row => {
          const idx = Number(row.dataset.logIndex);
          const block = blocks[idx];
          if (!block) return;
          if (row.classList.contains('is-delete-pending')) {
            deletedIndexes.add(idx);
            replacements.push({ start:block.sourceStart, end:block.sourceEnd, text:'' });
            return;
          }
          const value = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-date-full')?.value || '').trim();
          const events = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-unknown-events')?.value || '').trim();
          const body = String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-unknown-body')?.value || '').trim();
          if (!body) { invalidMessage = `${block.titleText}: 본문을 비울 수 없습니다. 필요 없는 항목은 삭제를 눌러 주세요.`; return; }
          const editedBlock = { ...block, events };
          let heading = formatNormalizedLogHeading(editedBlock);
          if (value) {
            const m = value.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
            if (!m) { invalidMessage = `${block.titleText}: 날짜 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.`; return; }
            heading = formatNormalizedLogHeading(editedBlock, Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]), Number(m[3]));
          } else if (row.querySelector('.rpcm-unknown-accepted')?.checked) {
            desiredAcceptedOldKeys.add(String(block.key));
          }
          const nextRaw = `${heading}\n${body}`;
          if (nextRaw !== block.raw) replacements.push({ start:block.sourceStart, end:block.sourceEnd, text:nextRaw });
        });

        if (invalidMessage) { notify(invalidMessage, 'warn', 5200); return; }
        const previousAccepted = [...new Set((room.acceptedUnknownLogKeys || []).map(String))].sort();
        const desiredAcceptedBeforeRemap = [...desiredAcceptedOldKeys].sort();
        const acceptanceChangedBeforeRemap = JSON.stringify(previousAccepted) !== JSON.stringify(desiredAcceptedBeforeRemap);
        if (!replacements.length && !acceptanceChangedBeforeRemap) { notify('변경된 내용이 없습니다.', 'info', 3200); return; }
        let next = originalText;
        replacements.sort((a,b) => b.start - a.start).forEach(r => { next = next.slice(0, r.start) + r.text + next.slice(r.end); });
        const newBlocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(next);
        const survivingBlocks = blocks.filter(block => !deletedIndexes.has(Number(block.index)));
        if (newBlocks.length !== survivingBlocks.length) { notify('수정 후 로그 블록 수가 예상과 달라져 적용을 중단했습니다.', 'error', 6200); return; }
        const liveLog = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
        if (!liveLog) { notify('현재 로그요약 항목을 찾지 못해 적용을 중단했습니다.', 'error', 6200); return; }
        liveLog.content = next;
        const keyMap = new Map();
