        survivingBlocks.forEach((block, index) => { if (newBlocks[index]) keyMap.set(String(block.key), String(newBlocks[index].key)); });
        remapLogSelectionKeys(room, keyMap, newBlocks);
        const newByKey = new Map(newBlocks.map(block => [String(block.key), block]));
        room.acceptedUnknownLogKeys = [...desiredAcceptedOldKeys]
          .map(key => keyMap.get(String(key)) || String(key))
          .filter(key => newByKey.get(String(key))?.isUnknown);
        finish(true);
      };
    });
  }

  function manualLogSelectionStats(room) {
    const log = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
    const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(log?.content || '');
    const selected = new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String));
    const items = blocks.filter(b => selected.has(String(b.key)));
    return {
      count: items.length,
      chars: items.reduce((n, b) => n + String(b.raw || '').length, 0),
      blocks: items,
    };
  }

  function openLogRecallManagerDialog(room) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const log = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
      const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(log?.content || '');
      if (!blocks.length) { notify('로그요약에서 날짜 블록을 찾지 못했습니다. [2026년 8월 31일-사건명]·[BC206-사건명] 같은 형식을 사용해 주세요.', 'warn', 6500); resolve(false); return; }
      const old = document.getElementById('rpcm-log-dialog-backdrop');
      if (old) old.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-log-dialog-backdrop';
      const pinned = new Set((room.autoLogPinnedKeys || []).map(String));
      const excluded = new Set((room.autoLogExcludedKeys || []).map(String));
      const manual = new Set((room.manualLogSelectedKeys || []).map(String));
      const favorite = new Set((room.favoriteLogKeys || []).map(String));
      const contextText = room.autoRecallContextText || '';
      const scored = new Map(scoreRelatedLogBlocks(blocks, contextText, new Set(), room).map(x => [x.block.key, x]));
      const timelineLabels = normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room).slice();
      const hasMultipleTimelines = timelineLabels.length > 1;
      const activeTimeline = activeLogTimelineLabel(room);
      const totalChars = blocks.reduce((n,b) => n + b.raw.length, 0);
      const timelineOptionHtml = selected => timelineLabels.map(label => `<option value="${esc(label)}" ${normalizedLogTimelineKey(label === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : label) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(selected === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : selected) ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(label)}</option>`).join('');
      const rowHtml = b => {
        const sc = scored.get(b.key);
        const reason = sc ? relatedLogReason(sc) : '현재 문맥 일치 없음';
        const timeline = logTimelineLabelOfBlock(b);
        return `<div class="rpcm-log-row${favorite.has(b.key) ? ' is-favorite' : ''}" data-log-key="${esc(b.key)}"><div class="rpcm-log-row-head"><strong>${esc(b.titleText)}</strong><span>${formatCount(b.raw.length)}자</span></div><div class="rpcm-log-row-reason">${esc(reason)}${sc ? ` · 점수 ${Number(sc.score).toFixed(1)}` : ''}</div><div class="rpcm-log-row-controls">${hasMultipleTimelines ? `<label class="rpcm-log-timeline-choice">이 블록 시간선 <select class="rpcm-log-timeline-select">${timelineOptionHtml(timeline)}</select></label>` : ''}<label><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-log-favorite" ${favorite.has(b.key) ? 'checked' : ''}> ★ 즐겨찾기</label><label title="자동 선택을 꺼도 이 날짜를 주입 후보에 포함합니다."><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-log-manual" ${manual.has(b.key) ? 'checked' : ''}> 직접 주입</label><label title="자동 선택 여부와 관계없이 이 날짜를 항상 우선 주입합니다."><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-log-pin" ${pinned.has(b.key) ? 'checked' : ''}> 📌 항상 주입</label><label title="최근·관련 로그 자동 선택에서 제외합니다. 직접 주입은 가능합니다."><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-log-exclude" ${excluded.has(b.key) ? 'checked' : ''}> 🚫 자동 선택 제외</label><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small rpcm-log-toggle">내용 보기</button></div><pre class="rpcm-log-content" hidden>${esc(b.raw)}</pre></div>`;
      };
      const timelineFolderHtml = (timelineLabel, timelineIndex) => {
        const timelineBlocks = blocks.filter(block => blockBelongsToTimeline(block, timelineLabel)).sort(compareLogBlocksChronologically);
        const grouped = new Map();
        const unknownBlocks = timelineBlocks.filter(b => b.isUnknown);
        const specialBlocks = timelineBlocks.filter(b => b.isSpecialDate);
        for (const b of timelineBlocks.filter(b => !b.isUnknown && !b.isSpecialDate)) {
          const yearLabel = b.year == null ? '연도 미상' : `${b.year}년`;
          if (!grouped.has(yearLabel)) grouped.set(yearLabel, new Map());
          const months = grouped.get(yearLabel);
          const monthLabel = `${b.month}월`;
          if (!months.has(monthLabel)) months.set(monthLabel, []);
          months.get(monthLabel).push(b);
        }
        const yearEntries = [...grouped.entries()];
        const latestYearIndex = yearEntries.length - 1;
        const dated = yearEntries.map(([yearLabel, months], yi) => {
          const monthEntries = [...months.entries()];
          return `<details class="rpcm-log-year" ${yi === latestYearIndex ? 'open' : ''}><summary><strong>${esc(yearLabel)}</strong><span>${[...months.values()].reduce((n,a)=>n+a.length,0)}개 날짜</span></summary>${monthEntries.map(([monthLabel, monthBlocks], mi) => {
            const weekGroups = [1,2,3,4,5].map(w => ({ w, arr:monthBlocks.filter(b => b.weekOfMonth === w) })).filter(x => x.arr.length);
            return `<details class="rpcm-log-month" ${yi === latestYearIndex && mi === monthEntries.length - 1 ? 'open' : ''}><summary><strong>${esc(monthLabel)}</strong><span>${monthBlocks.length}개 · ${formatCount(monthBlocks.reduce((n,b)=>n+b.raw.length,0))}자</span></summary><div class="rpcm-log-groupbar"><label><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-log-group-select" data-keys="${esc(monthBlocks.map(b=>b.key).join('|'))}"> 월 전체 직접 주입</label>${weekGroups.map(g => `<label><input type="checkbox" class="rpcm-log-group-select" data-keys="${esc(g.arr.map(b=>b.key).join('|'))}"> ${g.w}주 (${g.arr.length})</label>`).join('')}</div>${monthBlocks.map(rowHtml).join('')}</details>`;
          }).join('')}</details>`;
        }).join('');
        const special = specialBlocks.length ? `<details class="rpcm-log-year"><summary><strong>작품 고유 연호</strong><span>${specialBlocks.length}개</span></summary>${specialBlocks.map(rowHtml).join('')}</details>` : '';
        const unknown = unknownBlocks.length ? `<details class="rpcm-log-year"><summary><strong>날짜 미상</strong><span>${unknownBlocks.length}개</span></summary>${unknownBlocks.map(rowHtml).join('')}</details>` : '';
        const bodyHtml = `${unknown}${dated}${special}${timelineBlocks.length ? '' : '<div class="rpcm-log-empty">아직 저장된 날짜 블록이 없습니다.</div>'}`;
        if (!hasMultipleTimelines) return `<div class="rpcm-log-single-timeline">${bodyHtml}</div>`;
        const isActive = blockBelongsToTimeline({ timelineLabel:timelineLabel === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : timelineLabel }, activeTimeline);
        return `<details class="rpcm-log-timeline-folder" data-timeline-folder="${esc(timelineLabel)}" ${isActive ? 'open' : ''}><summary><strong>🗂️ ${esc(timelineLabel)}</strong><span>${isActive ? '다음 저장 · ' : ''}${timelineBlocks.length}개 블록 · ${formatCount(timelineBlocks.reduce((n,b)=>n+b.raw.length,0))}자</span></summary><div class="rpcm-log-timeline-body">${bodyHtml}</div></details>`;
      };
      const rowsHtml = timelineLabels.map(timelineFolderHtml).join('');

      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-log-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">날짜별 로그 저장소 · ${hasMultipleTimelines ? '시간선/주입 관리' : '주입 관리'}</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">원본 로그 ${blocks.length}개 블록 · ${formatCount(totalChars)}자 · ${hasMultipleTimelines ? '시간선→연도→월' : '연도→월'} 순서로 관리합니다.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          ${hasMultipleTimelines ? `<div class="rpcm-log-timeline-toolbar"><label><span>다음 요약 저장 시간선</span><select id="rpcm-active-log-timeline">${timelineOptionHtml(activeTimeline)}</select></label><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-add-log-timeline">＋ 새 시간선</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-rename-log-timeline">이름 변경</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small rpcm-log-timeline-delete" id="rpcm-delete-log-timeline">시간선 삭제</button></div><div class="rpcm-log-help"><b>활성 시간선은 다음 API 날짜요약이 이어서 저장될 시간선</b>만 정합니다. 최근·관련 로그 자동 선택은 현재 회차와 그보다 앞선 모든 시간선의 날짜를 함께 검색합니다. 활성 회차의 최신 날짜 1개는 직전 흐름으로 유지하고, 이전 회차는 사건명 등 강한 일치가 있을 때 관련 로그로 찾습니다.<br><b>기본 시간선</b>은 회귀 전 원래 전개이며 기존 ‘원래 시간선’ 표기도 이 폴더로 자동 합쳐집니다.</div>` : `<div class="rpcm-log-timeline-toolbar is-simple"><select id="rpcm-active-log-timeline" hidden>${timelineOptionHtml(activeTimeline)}</select><span class="rpcm-log-simple-label">날짜 로그</span><span class="rpcm-log-simple-desc">시간선이 필요한 경우에만 추가해 주세요.</span><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-small" id="rpcm-add-log-timeline">＋ 시간선 추가</button></div>`}
          <details class="rpcm-log-help-details"><summary>선택 상태 설명</summary><div class="rpcm-log-help"><b>★ 즐겨찾기</b>=나중에 빠르게 찾기 · <b>직접 주입</b>=다음 주입 후보에 강제 포함 · <b>📌 항상 주입</b>=항상 우선 포함 · <b>🚫 자동 선택 제외</b>=최근·관련 로그 자동 선택에서만 제외</div></details>
          <details class="rpcm-log-picked is-open" id="rpcm-log-picked" open><summary class="rpcm-log-picked-trigger" id="rpcm-log-picked-trigger" aria-expanded="true"><strong>즐겨찾기·항상 주입·직접 주입 로그</strong><span id="rpcm-log-picked-count"></span></summary><div id="rpcm-log-picked-body"></div></details>
          <div class="rpcm-log-help" id="rpcm-log-manager-summary"></div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-filterbar"><label>날짜·제목 검색<input id="rpcm-log-filter-query" type="search" placeholder="날짜나 사건 제목"></label><label>상태<select id="rpcm-log-filter-status"><option value="all">전체</option><option value="favorite">★ 즐겨찾기</option><option value="manual">직접 주입</option><option value="pinned">📌 항상 주입</option><option value="excluded">🚫 자동 제외</option></select></label><span id="rpcm-log-filter-count" role="status"></span></div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-filter-empty" id="rpcm-log-filter-empty" hidden>조건에 맞는 날짜로그가 없습니다.</div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-list">${rowsHtml}</div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" id="rpcm-log-clear-manual">직접 주입 전체 해제</button><div class="rpcm-spacer"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">적용</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);

      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      const managerSummary = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-manager-summary');
      const logFilterQuery = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-filter-query');
      const logFilterStatus = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-filter-status');
      const logFilterCount = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-filter-count');
      const logFilterEmpty = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-filter-empty');
      let logFilterOpenBefore = new Map();
      let logFilterWasActive = false;
      const logBlockByKey = new Map(blocks.map(block => [String(block.key), block]));
      const applyLogFilters = () => {
        const query = String(logFilterQuery.value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
        const status = logFilterStatus.value;
        const filtering = !!query || status !== 'all';
        const logFilterFolders = [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-list details')];
        if (filtering && !logFilterWasActive) logFilterOpenBefore = new Map(logFilterFolders.map(folder => [folder, folder.open]));
        let visible = 0;
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-list .rpcm-log-row').forEach(row => {
          const block = logBlockByKey.get(String(row.dataset.logKey || ''));
          const titleAndDate = `${block?.titleText || ''} ${block?.heading || ''} ${block?.fullDate || ''} ${logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block || {})}`.toLocaleLowerCase('ko-KR');
          const selector = { favorite:'.rpcm-log-favorite', manual:'.rpcm-log-manual', pinned:'.rpcm-log-pin', excluded:'.rpcm-log-exclude' }[status];
          const show = (!query || titleAndDate.includes(query)) && (!selector || !!row.querySelector(selector)?.checked);
          row.hidden = !show;
          if (show) visible++;
        });
        logFilterFolders.slice().reverse().forEach(folder => {
          folder.hidden = filtering && !folder.querySelector('.rpcm-log-row:not([hidden])');
          if (filtering) folder.open = !folder.hidden;
          else if (logFilterWasActive) folder.open = logFilterOpenBefore.get(folder) ?? folder.open;
        });
        logFilterWasActive = filtering;
        logFilterCount.textContent = `${visible}/${backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-list .rpcm-log-row').length}개 표시`;
        logFilterEmpty.hidden = visible !== 0;
      };
      logFilterQuery.addEventListener('input', applyLogFilters);
      logFilterStatus.addEventListener('change', applyLogFilters);
      backdrop.addEventListener('change', event => { if (event.target.matches('.rpcm-log-favorite,.rpcm-log-manual,.rpcm-log-pin,.rpcm-log-exclude')) applyLogFilters(); });
      const activeTimelineSelect = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-active-log-timeline');
      const pickedBody = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-picked-body');
      const pickedCount = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-picked-count');
      const pickedPanel = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-picked');
      const pickedTrigger = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-picked-trigger');
      const setPickedPanelOpen = open => {
        const isOpen = !!open;
        if (pickedPanel) pickedPanel.open = isOpen;
        pickedPanel?.classList.toggle('is-open', isOpen);
        pickedTrigger?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
        if (pickedBody) {
          pickedBody.hidden = false;
          pickedBody.removeAttribute('hidden');
        }
      };
      const deletedBlockKeys = new Set();
      const addTimelineOption = (label, selectIt = false) => {
        if (!sanitizeLogTimelineLabel(label)) return '';
        const clean = cleanLogTimelineLabel(label) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
        const existing = timelineLabels.find(item => normalizedLogTimelineKey(item === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : item) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(clean === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : clean));
        const resolved = existing || clean;
        if (!existing) {
          timelineLabels.push(resolved);
          backdrop.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-active-log-timeline,.rpcm-log-timeline-select').forEach(select => {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = resolved;
            option.textContent = resolved;
            select.appendChild(option);
          });
        }
        if (selectIt && activeTimelineSelect) activeTimelineSelect.value = resolved;
        return resolved;
      };
      const updateGroupState = () => {
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-group-select').forEach(group => {
          const keys = String(group.dataset.keys || '').split('|').filter(Boolean);
          const states = keys.map(k => !!backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key="${CSS.escape(k)}"] .rpcm-log-manual`)?.checked);
          group.checked = states.length > 0 && states.every(Boolean);
          group.indeterminate = states.some(Boolean) && !states.every(Boolean);
        });
        const selectedRows = [...backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-row')].filter(row => row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-manual')?.checked);
        const selectedChars = selectedRows.reduce((n, row) => {
          const key = String(row.dataset.logKey || '');
          const block = blocks.find(b => String(b.key) === key);
          return n + String(block?.raw || '').length;
        }, 0);
        if (managerSummary) managerSummary.innerHTML = `<b>현재 직접 주입 ${selectedRows.length}개</b> · ${formatCount(selectedChars)}자 · 최근·관련 로그 자동 선택을 꺼도 직접 주입 날짜는 주입 후보에 유지됩니다.`;
      };
      const refreshPickedManager = () => {
        const picked = blocks.map(block => {
          const row = backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key="${CSS.escape(String(block.key))}"]`);
          return { block, favorite:!!row?.querySelector('.rpcm-log-favorite')?.checked, manual:!!row?.querySelector('.rpcm-log-manual')?.checked, pinned:!!row?.querySelector('.rpcm-log-pin')?.checked };
        }).filter(item => item.favorite || item.manual || item.pinned);
        pickedPanel?.classList.toggle('is-empty', picked.length === 0);
        if (pickedCount) pickedCount.textContent = picked.length ? `${picked.length}개` : '0개 · 지정 없음';
        if (!pickedBody) return;
        pickedBody.innerHTML = picked.length ? picked.map(item => `<div class="rpcm-log-picked-row" data-picked-key="${esc(item.block.key)}"><div><strong>${item.favorite ? '★ ' : ''}${esc(item.block.titleText)}</strong><span>${esc(logTimelineLabelOfBlock(item.block))} · ${formatCount(item.block.raw.length)}자</span></div><div>${item.favorite ? '<button type="button" data-picked-clear="favorite">★ 해제</button>' : ''}${item.manual ? '<button type="button" data-picked-clear="manual">직접 주입 해제</button>' : '<button type="button" data-picked-enable="manual">직접 주입</button>'}${item.pinned ? '<button type="button" data-picked-clear="pinned">📌 해제</button>' : ''}<button type="button" data-picked-jump>날짜 보기</button></div></div>`).join('') : '<div class="rpcm-log-picked-empty">즐겨찾기나 지정 로그가 없습니다.</div>';
        setPickedPanelOpen(pickedPanel?.open ?? true);
        pickedBody.querySelectorAll('[data-picked-clear]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
          const key = String(button.closest('[data-picked-key]')?.dataset.pickedKey || '');
          const row = backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key="${CSS.escape(key)}"]`);
          const selector = button.dataset.pickedClear === 'manual' ? '.rpcm-log-manual' : button.dataset.pickedClear === 'favorite' ? '.rpcm-log-favorite' : '.rpcm-log-pin';
          const checkbox = row?.querySelector(selector);
          if (checkbox) { checkbox.checked = false; checkbox.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles:true })); }
          refreshPickedManager();
        });
        pickedBody.querySelectorAll('[data-picked-enable="manual"]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
          const key = String(button.closest('[data-picked-key]')?.dataset.pickedKey || '');
          const checkbox = backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key="${CSS.escape(key)}"] .rpcm-log-manual`);
          if (checkbox) { checkbox.checked = true; checkbox.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles:true })); }
          refreshPickedManager();
        });
        pickedBody.querySelectorAll('[data-picked-jump]').forEach(button => button.onclick = () => {
          const key = String(button.closest('[data-picked-key]')?.dataset.pickedKey || '');
          const row = backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key="${CSS.escape(key)}"]`);
          if (!row) return;
          let parent = row.parentElement;
          while (parent && parent !== backdrop) { if (parent.tagName === 'DETAILS') parent.open = true; parent = parent.parentElement; }
          row.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth', block:'center' });
          row.classList.add('is-picked-jump');
          setTimeout(() => row.classList.remove('is-picked-jump'), 1400);
        });
      };
      if (pickedPanel && pickedTrigger && pickedBody) pickedPanel.addEventListener('toggle', () => {
        const isOpen = !!pickedPanel.open;
        pickedPanel.classList.toggle('is-open', isOpen);
        pickedTrigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
        pickedBody.hidden = false;
        pickedBody.removeAttribute('hidden');
      });
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => finish(false);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(false);
      backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-add-log-timeline').onclick = () => {
        const label = prompt('새 시간선 이름을 입력해 주세요. 회차형이면 1회차·2회차처럼 관리할 수 있습니다.', nextLogTimelineRoundLabel(timelineLabels));
        if (label == null) return;
        const added = addTimelineOption(label, true);
        if (!added) notify('시간선 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3200);
        else if (!hasMultipleTimelines) notify(`‘${added}’ 시간선을 추가했습니다. 아래 적용을 누르면 다중 시간선 화면으로 바뀝니다.`, 'success', 4600);
      };
      const renameTimelineButton = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-rename-log-timeline');
      if (renameTimelineButton) renameTimelineButton.onclick = () => {
        const before = String(activeTimelineSelect?.value || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE);
        if (before === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE) { notify('기본 시간선 이름은 변경할 수 없습니다.', 'info', 3200); return; }
        const requested = prompt('시간선의 새 이름을 입력해 주세요.', before);
        if (requested == null) return;
        const after = cleanLogTimelineLabel(requested);
        if (!after) { notify('시간선 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3200); return; }
        if (timelineLabels.some(label => label !== before && normalizedLogTimelineKey(label === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE ? '' : label) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(after))) { notify('같은 이름의 시간선이 이미 있습니다.', 'warn', 3600); return; }
        const index = timelineLabels.indexOf(before);
        if (index >= 0) timelineLabels[index] = after;
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-active-log-timeline,.rpcm-log-timeline-select').forEach(select => {
          [...select.options].forEach(option => { if (option.value === before) { option.value = after; option.textContent = after; } });
        });
        activeTimelineSelect.value = after;
      };
      const deleteTimelineButton = backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-delete-log-timeline');
      if (deleteTimelineButton) deleteTimelineButton.onclick = () => {
        const target = String(activeTimelineSelect?.value || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE);
        if (target === DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE) {
          notify('기본 시간선은 삭제할 수 없습니다.', 'info', 3400);
          return;
        }
        const count = blocks.filter(block => blockBelongsToTimeline(block, target)).length;
        if (count) {
          if (!confirm(`‘${target}’ 시간선과 그 안의 날짜 블록 ${count}개를 함께 삭제할까요?\n아래 ‘적용’을 누르기 전에는 저장되지 않습니다.`)) return;
          blocks.filter(block => blockBelongsToTimeline(block, target)).forEach(block => deletedBlockKeys.add(String(block.key)));
        } else if (!confirm(`빈 시간선 ‘${target}’을 삭제할까요?`)) {
          return;
        }
        const index = timelineLabels.findIndex(label => normalizedLogTimelineKey(label) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(target));
        if (index >= 0) timelineLabels.splice(index, 1);
        backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-timeline-folder[data-timeline-folder="${CSS.escape(target)}"]`)?.remove();
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('#rpcm-active-log-timeline,.rpcm-log-timeline-select').forEach(select => {
          [...select.options].forEach(option => { if (normalizedLogTimelineKey(option.value) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(target)) option.remove(); });
        });
        const fallbackTimeline = timelineLabels[Math.max(0, index - 1)] || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
        activeTimelineSelect.value = fallbackTimeline;
        updateGroupState();
        refreshPickedManager();
        applyLogFilters();
        notify(`‘${target}’ 시간선${count ? `과 날짜 블록 ${count}개` : ''}을 삭제 대상으로 표시했습니다. 아래 적용을 눌러 저장해 주세요.`, 'success', 4800);
      };
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-toggle').forEach(btn => btn.onclick = () => {
        const pre = btn.closest('.rpcm-log-row')?.querySelector('.rpcm-log-content');
        if (!pre) return;
        pre.hidden = !pre.hidden;
        btn.textContent = pre.hidden ? '내용 보기' : '내용 닫기';
      });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-row').forEach(row => {
        const pin = row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-pin');
        const ex = row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-exclude');
        const man = row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-manual');
        const fav = row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-favorite');
        fav.onchange = () => { row.classList.toggle('is-favorite', fav.checked); refreshPickedManager(); };
        pin.onchange = () => { if (pin.checked) { ex.checked = false; setPickedPanelOpen(true); } updateGroupState(); refreshPickedManager(); };
        ex.onchange = () => { if (ex.checked) pin.checked = false; updateGroupState(); refreshPickedManager(); };
        man.onchange = () => { if (man.checked) setPickedPanelOpen(true); updateGroupState(); refreshPickedManager(); };
      });
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-group-select').forEach(group => group.onchange = () => {
        const desired = group.checked;
        for (const key of String(group.dataset.keys || '').split('|').filter(Boolean)) {
          const cb = backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key="${CSS.escape(key)}"] .rpcm-log-manual`);
          if (cb) cb.checked = desired;
        }
        if (desired) setPickedPanelOpen(true);
        updateGroupState();
        refreshPickedManager();
        applyLogFilters();
      });
      backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-log-clear-manual').onclick = () => {
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-manual').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = false; });
        updateGroupState();
        refreshPickedManager();
        applyLogFilters();
      };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const nextPinned = [], nextExcluded = [], nextManual = [], nextFavorite = [];
        const timelineByKey = new Map();
        backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-log-row').forEach(row => {
          const key = row.dataset.logKey;
          if (row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-pin')?.checked) nextPinned.push(key);
          if (row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-exclude')?.checked) nextExcluded.push(key);
          if (row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-manual')?.checked) nextManual.push(key);
          if (row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-favorite')?.checked) nextFavorite.push(key);
          timelineByKey.set(String(key), String(row.querySelector('.rpcm-log-timeline-select')?.value || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE));
        });
        room.autoLogPinnedKeys = nextPinned;
        room.autoLogExcludedKeys = nextExcluded;
        room.manualLogSelectedKeys = nextManual;
        room.favoriteLogKeys = nextFavorite;
        const replacements = blocks.filter(block => !deletedBlockKeys.has(String(block.key))).map(block => {
          const targetTimeline = timelineByKey.get(String(block.key)) || logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block);
          const nextHeading = formatLogHeadingForTimeline(block, targetTimeline);
          return nextHeading === block.heading ? null : { start:block.sourceStart, end:block.headingEnd, text:nextHeading };
        }).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b) => b.start - a.start);
        const deletions = blocks.filter(block => deletedBlockKeys.has(String(block.key))).map(block => ({ start:block.sourceStart, end:block.sourceEnd, text:'' }));
        const edits = [...replacements, ...deletions].sort((a,b) => b.start - a.start || b.end - a.end);
        if (edits.length) {
          let nextContent = String(log.content || '');
          edits.forEach(edit => { nextContent = nextContent.slice(0, edit.start) + edit.text + nextContent.slice(edit.end); });
          nextContent = sortDatedLogTextByTimeline(nextContent.trim(), room);
          const nextBlocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(nextContent);
          const survivingBlocks = blocks.filter(block => !deletedBlockKeys.has(String(block.key)));
          if (nextBlocks.length !== survivingBlocks.length) { notify('시간선 이동·삭제 후 블록 수가 예상과 달라져 적용을 중단했습니다.', 'error', 5200); return; }
          log.content = nextContent;
          const keyMap = new Map();
          const nextQueues = new Map();
          nextBlocks.forEach(block => {
            const signature = `${block.heading}\n${block.body}`;
            if (!nextQueues.has(signature)) nextQueues.set(signature, []);
            nextQueues.get(signature).push(block);
          });
          survivingBlocks.forEach(block => {
            const targetTimeline = timelineByKey.get(String(block.key)) || logTimelineLabelOfBlock(block);
            const signature = `${formatLogHeadingForTimeline(block, targetTimeline)}\n${block.body}`;
            const nextBlock = nextQueues.get(signature)?.shift();
            if (nextBlock) keyMap.set(String(block.key), String(nextBlock.key));
          });
          remapLogSelectionKeys(room, keyMap, nextBlocks);
        }
        room.logTimelines = timelineLabels.slice();
        room.activeLogTimeline = cleanLogTimelineLabel(activeTimelineSelect?.value) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
        normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room);
        finish(true);
      };
      updateGroupState();
      refreshPickedManager();
      applyLogFilters();
      backdrop.onclick = e => { if (e.target === backdrop) finish(false); };
    });
  }

  function openDuplicateLogResolverDialog(room) {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const log = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
      const blocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(log?.content || '');
      const groups = duplicateLogDateGroups(room);
      if (!groups.length) { notify('현재 중복 날짜 로그가 없습니다.', 'success', 3200); resolve(false); return; }

      const old = document.getElementById('rpcm-dup-dialog-backdrop');
      if (old) old.remove();
      const backdrop = document.createElement('div');
      backdrop.id = 'rpcm-dup-dialog-backdrop';
      const branchHint = groups.map(timelineBranchHintForGroup).find(Boolean) || '';

      const groupsHtml = groups.map((group, gi) => {
        const hint = timelineBranchHintForGroup(group);
        return `
        <div class="rpcm-dup-group" data-date-key="${esc(group.dateKey)}">
          <div class="rpcm-dup-group-head"><strong>${esc(group.label)}</strong>${hint ? `<em class="rpcm-timeline-hint">↩ 분기 신호 감지 · ${esc(hint)}</em>` : ''}<span>${group.blocks.length}개 블록 감지 · 유지할 블록 하나를 선택하세요.</span></div>
          ${group.blocks.map((b, bi) => `
            <div class="rpcm-dup-choice ${bi === group.blocks.length - 1 ? 'is-selected' : ''}" data-block-index="${b.index}">
              <label class="rpcm-dup-choice-head">
                <input type="radio" name="rpcm-dup-${gi}" value="${b.index}" ${bi === group.blocks.length - 1 ? 'checked' : ''}>
                <strong>${bi + 1}번째 블록${bi === group.blocks.length - 1 ? ' · 기본 선택' : ''}</strong>
                <span>${formatCount(b.raw.length)}자</span>
              </label>
              <label class="rpcm-dup-heading"><span>날짜 · 키워드 제목</span><input type="text" value="${esc(b.heading)}" placeholder="[2027년 10월 4일-키워드]" spellcheck="false"></label>
              <textarea class="rpcm-dup-editor" spellcheck="false">${esc(b.body)}</textarea>
            </div>`).join('')}
        </div>`;
      }).join('');

      backdrop.innerHTML = `
        <div class="rpcm-log-dialog rpcm-dup-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-head"><div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-title">중복 날짜 로그 정리</div><div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-desc">같은 시간선·날짜로 감지된 블록입니다. 실제 중복이면 하나를 남기고, 시간회귀·IF 분기라면 각각 다른 시간선 폴더로 보관하세요.</div></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-lib-close" aria-label="닫기" data-rpcm-close="icon">✕</button></div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-help">${branchHint ? `<b>↩ 시간선 분기 가능성을 감지했습니다.</b> ‘${esc(branchHint)}’ 문구가 있습니다. 자동 확정하지 않으니 아래 블록을 확인해 주세요.<br>` : ''}<b>일반 중복</b>=유지할 블록 하나를 선택 · <b>시간선 분기</b>=첫 블록은 기본 시간선, 다음 블록은 회귀 시간선 또는 추가 회차로 나눠 보관</div>
          <div class="rpcm-log-list rpcm-dup-list">${groupsHtml}</div>
          <div class="rpcm-lib-dialog-actions"><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn ${branchHint ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}" id="rpcm-split-duplicate-timelines">${branchHint ? '↩ 분기 감지 · ' : '🗂️ '}시간선 폴더로 나누기</button><div class="rpcm-spacer"></div><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-act="cancel">취소</button><button type="button" class="rpcm-btn primary" data-act="confirm">선택한 블록으로 정리</button></div>
        </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(backdrop);

      const finish = value => { backdrop.remove(); resolve(value); };
      backdrop.querySelector('.rpcm-lib-close').onclick = () => finish(false);
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="cancel"]').onclick = () => finish(false);
      backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-dup-choice input[type="radio"]').forEach(radio => {
        radio.onchange = () => {
          const group = radio.closest('.rpcm-dup-group');
          group?.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-dup-choice').forEach(choice => choice.classList.toggle('is-selected', !!choice.querySelector('input[type="radio"]')?.checked));
        };
      });
      backdrop.querySelector('#rpcm-split-duplicate-timelines').onclick = () => {
        const branchCount = Math.max(...groups.map(group => group.blocks.length));
        const labels = [DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE];
        for (let index = 1; index < branchCount; index++) {
          const fallback = index === 1 ? DEFAULT_REGRESSION_TIMELINE : `${index}회차`;
          const value = prompt(`${index + 1}번째 블록이 들어갈 시간선 이름`, fallback);
          if (value == null) return;
          const label = cleanLogTimelineLabel(value) || DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE;
          if (!label) { notify('시간선 이름을 입력해 주세요.', 'warn', 3200); return; }
          if (labels.some(item => normalizedLogTimelineKey(item) === normalizedLogTimelineKey(label))) { notify('시간선 이름은 서로 달라야 합니다.', 'warn', 3600); return; }
          labels.push(label);
        }
        const targetByIndex = new Map();
        groups.forEach(group => group.blocks.forEach((block, index) => targetByIndex.set(block.index, labels[index])));
        const replacements = blocks.filter(block => targetByIndex.has(block.index)).map(block => ({
          start:block.sourceStart,
          end:block.headingEnd,
          text:formatLogHeadingForTimeline(block, targetByIndex.get(block.index)),
        })).sort((a,b) => b.start - a.start);
        let next = String(log?.content || '');
        replacements.forEach(replacement => { next = next.slice(0, replacement.start) + replacement.text + next.slice(replacement.end); });
        const nextBlocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(next);
        if (nextBlocks.length !== blocks.length) { notify('시간선 분리 후 블록 수가 달라져 적용을 중단했습니다.', 'error', 5200); return; }
        log.content = next;
        remapLogSelectionKeysByIndex(room, blocks, nextBlocks);
        room.logTimelines = [...new Set([...(room.logTimelines || []), ...labels])];
        room.activeLogTimeline = labels[labels.length - 1] || activeLogTimelineLabel(room);
        normalizeRoomLogTimelines(room);
        finish(true);
      };
      backdrop.querySelector('[data-act="confirm"]').onclick = () => {
        const selectedByDate = new Map();
        for (const group of groups) {
          const groupEl = backdrop.querySelector(`.rpcm-dup-group[data-date-key="${CSS.escape(group.dateKey)}"]`);
          const selected = groupEl?.querySelector('input[type="radio"]:checked');
          if (!selected) { notify(`${group.label}: 유지할 블록을 선택해 주세요.`, 'warn', 4200); return; }
          const choice = selected.closest('.rpcm-dup-choice');
          const rawHeading = String(choice?.querySelector('.rpcm-dup-heading input')?.value || '').replace(/[\r\n]+/g, ' ').trim();
          const innerHeading = rawHeading.replace(/^\[/, '').replace(/\]$/, '').trim();
          const heading = /^\[[^\]\n]+\]$/.test(rawHeading) ? rawHeading : innerHeading ? `[${innerHeading}]` : '';
          const body = String(choice?.querySelector('.rpcm-dup-editor')?.value || '').trim();
          const edited = `${heading}${body ? `\n${body}` : ''}`.trim();
          if (!heading || parseDatedLogBlocks(edited).length !== 1) {
            notify(`${group.label}: 날짜 제목을 [2027년 10월 4일-키워드] 형태로 입력해 주세요.`, 'warn', 5200);
            return;
          }
          selectedByDate.set(group.dateKey, { index: Number(selected.value), text: edited });
        }

        const src = normalizeLineBreaks(log?.content || '');
        const prefix = blocks.length && blocks[0].sourceStart > 0 ? src.slice(0, blocks[0].sourceStart).trim() : '';
        const duplicateDates = new Set(groups.map(g => g.dateKey));
        const pieces = prefix ? [prefix] : [];
        const pieceOriginKeys = [];
        for (const block of blocks) {
          if (!duplicateDates.has(block.dateKey)) {
            pieces.push(String(block.raw || '').trim());
            pieceOriginKeys.push(String(block.key));
            continue;
          }
          const chosen = selectedByDate.get(block.dateKey);
          if (chosen?.index === block.index) {
            pieces.push(chosen.text);
            pieceOriginKeys.push(String(block.key));
          }
        }
        const liveLog = (room.slots || []).find(s => s.id === 'logSummary');
        if (!liveLog) { notify('현재 로그요약 항목을 찾지 못해 적용을 중단했습니다.', 'error', 6200); return; }
        liveLog.content = pieces.filter(Boolean).join('\n\n').trim();
        const nextBlocks = parseDatedLogBlocks(liveLog.content);
        const keyMap = new Map();
        pieceOriginKeys.forEach((origin, index) => {
          if (nextBlocks[index]) keyMap.set(origin, String(nextBlocks[index].key));
        });
        remapLogSelectionKeys(room, keyMap, nextBlocks);
        finish(true);
      };
      backdrop.onclick = e => { if (e.target === backdrop) finish(false); };
    });
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // IndexedDB
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  function openDb() {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const req = indexedDB.open(APP.dbName, APP.dbVersion);
      req.onupgradeneeded = () => {
        const db = req.result;
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(APP.storeName)) {
          db.createObjectStore(APP.storeName, { keyPath: 'chatId' });
        }
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(APP.libraryStoreName)) {
          db.createObjectStore(APP.libraryStoreName, { keyPath: 'scopeId' });
        }
      };
      req.onsuccess = () => {const db=req.result;db.onversionchange=()=>{db.close();notify('저장소가 다른 탭에서 변경되었습니다. 새로고침 후 다시 저장하세요.','warn',8000);};resolve(db);};
      req.onblocked=()=>notify('다른 RP Manager 탭이 저장소를 사용 중입니다. 다른 탭을 닫고 다시 시도하세요.','warn',8000);
      req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    });
  }

  function dbTx(mode, fn) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = state.db.transaction(APP.storeName, mode);
      const store = tx.objectStore(APP.storeName);
      let result;
      try {
        result = fn(store);
      } catch (e) {
        reject(e);
        return;
      }
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(result);
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
      tx.onabort = () => reject(tx.error || new Error('DB transaction aborted'));
    });
  }

  async function getRoom(chatId, apiChatId = null) {
    const room = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = state.db.transaction(APP.storeName, 'readonly');
      const req = tx.objectStore(APP.storeName).get(chatId);
      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || null);
      req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    });

    if (room) {
      rpcmRememberRoomBaseline(room);
      const needsStoryLogMigration = !room.storyLogReviewRevisionV1 || !room.storyTimelineGuideRevisionV15;
      if (!Array.isArray(room.lorePacks)) room.lorePacks = [];
      normalizeRoomSlots(room);
      const oldPersonBuiltin = isLegacyBuiltinPersonGuide(room.personStateGuide);
      if (oldPersonBuiltin) room.personStateGuide = canonicalGuideText('personInfo');
      const oldLoreBuiltin = [PREVIOUS_MANUAL_LORE_GUIDE_V2_0_1,PREVIOUS_CANONICAL_GUIDES_V01456.lore].includes(room.manualUpdateGuides?.lore);
      if (oldLoreBuiltin) delete room.manualUpdateGuides.lore;
      const oldTimelineBuiltin = !room.storyTimelineGuide || room.storyTimelineGuide === STORY_TIMELINE_GUIDE_V15 || room.storyTimelineGuide === LEGACY_CANONICAL_GUIDES.timeline || room.storyTimelineGuide === PREVIOUS_CANONICAL_GUIDES_V01456.timeline;
      if (oldTimelineBuiltin) {
        room.storyTimelineGuide = canonicalGuideText('timeline');
        room.storyTimelineGuideSource = 'builtin-v1.7.2';
      }
      normalizeAiReviewInbox(room);
      room.maxChars = APP.defaultMaxChars;
      room.apiChatId = room.apiChatId || apiChatId || String(chatId).split('::')[0];
      if (room.pending) {
        room.pending.contextBlock = String(room.pending.contextBlock || '');
        room.pending.sessionStartedAt = Number(room.pending.sessionStartedAt || room.pending.armedAt || Date.now());
        normalizePendingSlotMetadata(room);
        if (!Array.isArray(room.pending.items) || !room.pending.items.length) {
          const legacyTotal = room.pending.totalTurns == null ? APP.defaultRetentionTurns : normalizeRetentionTurns(room.pending.totalTurns);
          const legacyUsed = Number(room.pending.usedTurns || 0);
          room.pending.items = selectedSlots(room).map(slot => ({
            slotId: slot.id, title: slot.title, group: slot.group, content: String(slot.content || ''),
            totalTurns: normalizeRetentionTurns(slot.retentionTurns ?? legacyTotal), usedTurns: legacyUsed,
          }));
        }
      }
      if (needsStoryLogMigration || oldPersonBuiltin || oldLoreBuiltin || oldTimelineBuiltin) await saveRoom(room);
      return room;
    }

    const created = {
      chatId,
      apiChatId: apiChatId || String(chatId).split('::')[0],
      label: '',
      maxChars: APP.defaultMaxChars,
      slots: cloneSlots(),
      pending: null,
      autoCharacterDetection: false,
      autoCharacterLibraryId: '',
      lastExtraLibraryId: '',
      autoCharacterResetOnReappear: true,
      autoItemDetection: true,
      autoItemResetOnReappear: true,
      allowedDuplicateItemSignatures: [],
      autoLogRecallEnabled: false,
      autoLogRecentBlocks: APP.defaultRecentLogBlocks,
      autoLogRelevance: 'balanced',
      autoLogRelatedBlocks: APP.defaultRelatedLogBlocks,
      autoLogPinnedKeys: [],
      autoLogExcludedKeys: [],
      manualLogSelectedKeys: [],
      favoriteLogKeys: [],
      acceptedUnknownLogKeys: [],
      aiContextLogRerankEnabled: false,
      aiApiUsage: { version:1, features:{ summary:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), timeline:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), context:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), itemFolder:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), utility:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), longTermManualFirst:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), longTermAutoFirst:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), longTermSecond:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage(), longTermNative:emptyRoomAiFeatureUsage() }, history:[] },
      logTimelines: [DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE],
      activeLogTimeline: DEFAULT_LOG_TIMELINE,
      storyTimelineCards: [],
      lorePacks: [],
      userCanonMemos: [],
      knowledgeCards: [],
      knowledgeInjectionEnabled: false,
      personStateRecords: [],
      personStateInjectionEnabled: false,
      personPlayerName: '',
      personBundleV01411: true,
      storyTimelineInjectionEnabled: true,
      storyLogReviews: [],
      storyTimelineGuide: canonicalGuideText('timeline'),
      storyTimelineGuideSource: 'builtin-v1.7.2',
      storyTimelineGuideRevisionV15: true,
      storyTimelinePendingReview: null,
      storyLogReviewRevisionV1: true,
      storyTimelineLastReviewedMessageId: '',
      storyTimelineLastReviewedAt: '',
      storyTimelineReviewSettings: { threshold:50, menuBadge:true, topNotice:true, popup:false, dateIndicator:true, monthlyFold:true, monthlyFoldMin:8 },
      storyTimelineLastDiff: [],
      storyTimelineBackup: null,
      storyTimelineApiDraft: null,
      storyTimelineApiHistory: [],
      storyTimelineAuditHistory: [],
      storyTimelineManualRun: null,
      storyTimelineCarryoverTurnKeys: [],
      storyTimelineGuideRevisionV14: true,
      storyTimelineGuideFullRevisionV14: true,
      autoScanLastMessageId: '',
      autoRecallContextText: '',
      aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKey: '',
      aiSummaryLastAppliedAt: '',
      aiSummaryLastAppliedTurnKeys: { logSummary:'', currentState:'' },
      aiSummaryLastAppliedAts: { logSummary:'', currentState:'' },
      aiSummaryUndo: null,
      aiSummaryDraft: null,
      aiSummaryHistory: [],
      aiReviewInbox: [],
      createdAt: nowIso(),
      updatedAt: nowIso(),
    };
    await saveRoom(created);
    return created;
  }

  function updateSaveStatusUi(status = state.saveStatus) {
    state.saveStatus = status;
    const el = state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-save-status');
    if (!el) return;
    if (status === 'saving') { el.textContent = '로컬 저장 중…'; el.className = 'rpcm-save-status saving'; return; }
    if (status === 'error') { el.textContent = '로컬 저장 실패'; el.className = 'rpcm-save-status error'; return; }
    const when = state.lastSavedAt ? new Date(state.lastSavedAt).toLocaleTimeString('ko-KR', { hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit', second:'2-digit' }) : '';
    el.textContent = when ? `로컬 저장됨 · ${when}` : '로컬 저장됨';
    el.className = 'rpcm-save-status saved';
  }

  function renderCarrierSyncStatusUi(room = state.currentRoom) {
    const el = state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-carrier-status');
    if (!el) return;
    el.hidden = !room?.pending;
    if (!room?.pending) return;
    const sessionKey = String(room.pending.sessionStartedAt || room.pending.armedAt || room.pending.messageId || '');
    const saved = state.carrierSyncStatuses.get(String(room.chatId));
    const entry = saved?.sessionKey === sessionKey ? saved : { phase:'idle' };
    const labels = { idle:'현재 주입 유지 중', queued:'현재 주입 반영 대기…', syncing:'현재 주입 반영 중…', applied:'현재 주입 반영됨', suspended:'현재 주입 복구 대기', error:'현재 주입 반영 실패' };
    el.textContent = labels[entry.phase] || labels.idle;
    el.className = `rpcm-carrier-status ${entry.phase}`;
    el.title = entry.phase === 'error' ? '로컬 저장 상태와 별개로 현재 주입 갱신에 실패했습니다. 화면의 오류 알림을 확인해 주세요.' : '현재 주입 중인 carrier의 반영 상태';
    const arm = state.modal?.querySelector('#rpcm-arm');
    const armLabel = arm?.querySelector('.rpcm-footer-arm-label');
    if (armLabel) {
      armLabel.textContent = entry.phase === 'error' ? '반영 오류' : entry.phase === 'syncing' || entry.phase === 'queued' ? '재검증 중' : room.pending.reanchorRetry ? '복구 대기' : room.pending.verified ? '주입 중' : '서버 확인 필요';
      arm.title = `${armLabel.textContent} · 누르면 주입 해제`;
    }
  }

  function setCarrierSyncStatusUi(room, phase) {
    if (!room?.chatId) return 0;
    const key = String(room.chatId);
    const revision = (state.carrierSyncStatuses.get(key)?.revision || 0) + 1;
    const sessionKey = String(room.pending?.sessionStartedAt || room.pending?.armedAt || room.pending?.messageId || '');
    state.carrierSyncStatuses.set(key, { phase, revision, sessionKey });
    if (key === String(state.currentChatId)) renderCarrierSyncStatusUi(room);
    return revision;
  }

  function finishCarrierSyncStatusUi(room, revision, phase) {
    const key = String(room?.chatId || '');
    if (state.carrierSyncStatuses.get(key)?.revision !== revision) return;
    const sessionKey = String(room.pending?.sessionStartedAt || room.pending?.armedAt || room.pending?.messageId || '');
    state.carrierSyncStatuses.set(key, { phase, revision, sessionKey });
    if (key === String(state.currentChatId)) renderCarrierSyncStatusUi(room);
  }

  // Keep the live input element intact while typing, including Korean IME.
  const rpcmComposingEditors = new Set();
  function rpcmHasActiveComposition() {
    for (const editor of rpcmComposingEditors) if (!editor.isConnected) rpcmComposingEditors.delete(editor);
    return rpcmComposingEditors.size > 0;
  }
  function rpcmTextEditor(node) {
    return !!node?.matches?.('textarea,input:not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]):not([type=button]):not([type=submit]):not([type=file]):not([type=hidden]),[contenteditable=true]');
  }
  let rpcmEditingGuardsBound = false;
  function rpcmBindEditingGuards() {
    if (rpcmEditingGuardsBound) return;
    rpcmEditingGuardsBound = true;
    const owned = node => rpcmTextEditor(node) && !!node.closest('[id^="rpcm-"],[id^="wish-rpcore-"]');
    const flush = () => requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      const overlay = state.modal;
      if (!overlay?._rpcmDeferredEditorRender || rpcmHasActiveComposition()
          || (overlay.contains(document.activeElement) && rpcmTextEditor(document.activeElement))) return;
      overlay._rpcmDeferredEditorRender = false;
      renderModalIfOpen();
    });
    document.addEventListener('compositionstart', event => {
      if (owned(event.target)) rpcmComposingEditors.add(event.target);
    }, true);
    document.addEventListener('compositionend', event => {
      if (!rpcmComposingEditors.delete(event.target)) return;
      // The final input event follows compositionend in some browsers.
      if (state.currentRoom && state.modal?.contains(event.target)) queueRoomAutoSave(state.currentRoom, 150);
      flush();
    }, true);
    document.addEventListener('focusout', event => {
      if (owned(event.target)) flush();
    }, true);
  }

  function queueRoomAutoSave(room, delay = 500) {
    invalidateAiMemoryHistory();
    if (!room) return;
    const key = String(room.chatId || '');
    const previous = state.autoSaveTimers.get(key);
    if (previous) clearTimeout(previous);
    updateSaveStatusUi('saving');
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      state.autoSaveTimers.delete(key);
      if (rpcmHasActiveComposition()) { queueRoomAutoSave(room, 200); return; }
      try { await saveRoom(room); }
      catch (e) { updateSaveStatusUi('error'); console.warn('[🪽위시 RP Manager] 자동저장 실패', e); }
    }, delay);
    state.autoSaveTimers.set(key, timer);
  }


  // UI-only metadata stays outside room data, AI evidence, and commit hashes.
  const rpcmNewBadgeCache = new Map();
  let rpcmNewBadgeFrame = 0;
  function rpcmNewBadgeState(room) {
    const id=String(room?.chatId||'');
    if(!rpcmNewBadgeCache.has(id)){
      let saved;try{saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('RPCM_new_items_v1:'+id)||'null');}catch(_){}
      rpcmNewBadgeCache.set(id,{clock:Number(saved?.clock)||0,lastTime:Number(saved?.lastTime)||0,seen:Array.isArray(saved?.seen)?saved.seen.slice(-64):[],items:saved?.items&&typeof saved.items==='object'?saved.items:{},inventory:Array.isArray(saved?.inventory)?saved.inventory:null});
    }
    return rpcmNewBadgeCache.get(id);
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgePersist(room,meta) {
    try{localStorage.setItem('RPCM_new_items_v1:'+String(room.chatId),JSON.stringify(meta));}catch(_){}
    rpcmNewBadgeSchedule();
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeStamp(room,keys) {
    if(!room?.chatId||!keys.length)return;
    const meta=rpcmNewBadgeState(room);let changed=false;
    for(const key of keys)if(key&&!Object.hasOwn(meta.items,key)){meta.items[key]=meta.clock;changed=true;}
    if(changed)rpcmNewBadgePersist(room,meta);
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeEntityKeys(room) {
    const keys=new Set(),add=(type,rows,id='id')=>{for(const row of rows||[])if(row?.[id])keys.add(type+':'+String(row[id]));};
    add('slot',room.slots);add('knowledge',room.knowledgeCards);add('person',room.personStateRecords);add('timeline',room.storyTimelineCards,'cardId');add('memo',room.userCanonMemos);
    const actor=name=>{if(String(name||'').trim())keys.add('actor:'+String(name).trim());};
    for(const row of room.personUiActors||[])actor(row.name);
    for(const row of room.slots||[])if(row.group==='character')actor(row.title);
    for(const row of room.knowledgeCards||[])actor(row.person);
    for(const row of room.personStateRecords||[]){actor(row.person);actor(row.target);}
    for(const pack of room.lorePacks||[]){if(pack.id)keys.add('pack:'+pack.id);for(const card of pack.cards||[])if(card.id)keys.add('lore:'+pack.id+':'+card.id);}
    const log=(room.slots||[]).find(slot=>slot.id==='logSummary');
    for(const block of parseDatedLogBlocks(log?.content||''))keys.add('log:'+block.key);
    return keys;
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeCommitted(room,before,writer='') {
    // Initial room creation, migrations, undo, and backup restoration are not new updates.
    if(!before?.chatId||/migrat|restor|undo|rollback/i.test(writer))return;
    const previous=rpcmNewBadgeEntityKeys(before),next=rpcmNewBadgeEntityKeys(room);
    rpcmNewBadgeStamp(room,[...next].filter(key=>!previous.has(key)));
    for(const slot of room.slots||[])if(String(slot.content||'').trim()&&!String((before.slots||[]).find(row=>row.id===slot.id)?.content||'').trim())rpcmNewBadgeStamp(room,['slot:'+slot.id]);
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeInventory(room,inventory) {
    const meta=rpcmNewBadgeState(room),ids=inventory.map(longTermId).filter(Boolean);
    if(meta.inventory){const old=new Set(meta.inventory);rpcmNewBadgeStamp(room,ids.filter(id=>!old.has(id)).map(id=>'longterm:'+id));}
    if(JSON.stringify(meta.inventory)!==JSON.stringify(ids)){meta.inventory=ids;rpcmNewBadgePersist(room,meta);}
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeObserveMessages(chatId,messages,chronological=false) {
    const room=state.currentRoom;if(!room||String(apiChatIdOf(room))!==String(chatId))return;
    const ordered=chronological?messages:[...messages].reverse(),assistantToUser=new Map(),times=new Map();let userId='';
    for(const message of ordered){const role=messageRoleOf(message);if(role==='user'){userId=String(messageIdOf(message)||message.turnId||'');const time=Date.parse(message.createdAt||message.created_at||'')||(/^[0-9a-f]{24}$/i.test(userId)?parseInt(userId.slice(0,8),16)*1000:0);times.set(userId,time);}else if(userId&&['assistant','character','bot'].includes(role))assistantToUser.set(String(messageIdOf(message)||''),userId);}
    const keys=[...new Set(buildCompletedAiDialogueTurns(ordered).map(turn=>assistantToUser.get(turn.reviewSources?.[0]?.id)||String(turn.key)))];
    if(!keys.length)return;
    const meta=rpcmNewBadgeState(room);
    if(!meta.seen.length){meta.seen=keys.slice(-64);meta.lastTime=times.get(keys.at(-1))||0;rpcmNewBadgePersist(room,meta);return;}
    const anchor=keys.lastIndexOf(meta.seen.at(-1));
    // Older/out-of-order pages cannot advance the clock. Re-edits and rerolls share a user ID.
    const seen=new Set(meta.seen),fresh=(anchor>=0?keys.slice(anchor+1):meta.lastTime?keys.filter(key=>(times.get(key)||0)>meta.lastTime):[]).filter(key=>!seen.has(key));
    if(!fresh.length)return;
    meta.clock+=fresh.length;meta.seen=[...meta.seen,...fresh].slice(-64);
    meta.lastTime=Math.max(meta.lastTime,...fresh.map(key=>times.get(key)||0));
    for(const [key,born] of Object.entries(meta.items))if(meta.clock-born>100)delete meta.items[key];
    rpcmNewBadgePersist(room,meta);
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeRemaining(room,key) {
    const meta=rpcmNewBadgeState(room);return Object.hasOwn(meta.items,key)?Math.max(0,5-(meta.clock-meta.items[key])):0;
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgeSchedule() {
    if(rpcmNewBadgeFrame)return;
    rpcmNewBadgeFrame=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{rpcmNewBadgeFrame=0;rpcmNewBadgePaint();});
  }
  function rpcmNewBadgePaint() {
    const room=state.currentRoom;if(!room)return;
    const specs=[
      ['.rpcm-slot[data-slot-id]','slot','slotId','summary .rpcm-slot-title,summary strong,summary .rpcm-slot-name'],
      ['.rpcm-knowledge-card[data-knowledge-id]','knowledge','knowledgeId','summary strong'],
      ['.rpcm-person-record[data-person-record]','person','personRecord','summary strong'],
      ['.m3-panel[data-new-knowledge-ids]','knowledge-set','newKnowledgeIds','.m3-row b'],
      ['.m3-panel[data-new-person-id]','person','newPersonId','.m3-row b'],
      ['.m3-who[data-new-actor]','actor','newActor','.m3-person-open b'],
      ['.rpcm-profile[data-person-name]','actor','personName','summary strong'],
      ['.rpcm-user-memo-card[data-memo-id]','memo','memoId','.rpcm-user-memo-row strong'],
      ['[data-inline-story-id]','timeline','inlineStoryId','summary strong'],
      ['.rpcm-story-card[data-story-card-id]','timeline','storyCardId','.rpcm-story-card-top'],
      ['.rpcm-lore-pack[data-lore-pack]','pack','lorePack','summary strong'],
      ['.rpcm-lore-card[data-lore-card]','lore','loreCard','summary strong'],
      ['.rpcm-ltm-memory[data-ltm-card-id]','longterm','ltmCardId','label strong'],
      ['.rpcm-log-row[data-log-key],.rpcm-detached-log-card[data-log-key]','log','logKey','.rpcm-log-row-head strong,summary strong,h3,strong'],
    ];
    for(const [selector,type,attr,heading] of specs)for(const node of document.querySelectorAll(selector)){
      const surface=node.closest('[data-room-id]');if(surface&&surface.dataset.roomId!==String(room.chatId))continue;
      let key=type+':'+(type==='lore'?(node.closest('[data-lore-pack]')?.dataset.lorePack||'')+':':'')+node.dataset[attr];
      if(type==='knowledge-set'){const ids=JSON.parse(node.dataset[attr]||'[]');key=ids.map(id=>'knowledge:'+id).find(id=>rpcmNewBadgeRemaining(room,id)>0)||'knowledge-set:expired';for(const old of node.querySelectorAll('[data-rpcm-new-key]'))if(old.dataset.rpcmNewKey!==key)old.remove();}
      const remaining=rpcmNewBadgeRemaining(room,key),existing=node.querySelector('[data-rpcm-new-key="'+CSS.escape(key)+'"]');
      if(!remaining){existing?.remove();continue;}
      const target=node.querySelector(heading);if(!target)continue;
      const badge=existing||document.createElement('span');badge.className='rpcm-new-badge';badge.dataset.rpcmNewKey=key;badge.textContent='NEW';badge.title='새로 추가됨 · 완료 RP '+remaining+'턴 뒤 표시 종료';badge.setAttribute('aria-label',badge.title);
      if(!existing)target.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',badge);
    }
  }

  // Separate from site IndexedDB: best-effort userscript-manager recovery copies.
  // Never includes API credentials (they live in separate authentication settings).
  const rescueRuntime={queue:Promise.resolve(),error:'',seeded:false,pending:new Map(),timer:null};
  function rescueKey(kind,id=''){return `RPCM_RESCUE_V1:${location.origin}:${kind}:${id}`;}
  function rescueUseful(kind,value){return kind==='library'?!!value?.scopeId&&((value.characters||[]).length||(value.extras||[]).length):!!value?.chatId&&((value.slots||[]).some(s=>String(s.content||'').trim())||(value.knowledgeCards||[]).length||(value.personStateRecords||[]).length||(value.storyTimelineCards||[]).length||(value.lorePacks||[]).length||(value.userCanonMemos||[]).length);}
  function rescueCopy(value){const copy=structuredClone(value);copy.pending=null;for(const key of ['fullRebuildJob','manualIntegratedJob','manualPendingJobs','manualFullBuild','compressionJob','compressionDraft','storyTimelineApiDraft'])delete copy[key];return copy;}
  async function rescueStore(kind,value){
    if(!rescueUseful(kind,value))return;const id=String(kind==='room'?value.chatId:value.scopeId),data=rescueCopy(value),hash=await longTermHash(JSON.stringify(data));
    const key=rescueKey(kind,id),previous=await GM_getValue(key,null);let prior=null;try{prior=typeof previous==='string'?JSON.parse(previous):previous;}catch(_){}
    const content=copy=>{const data=structuredClone(copy);for(const key of ['_rpcmRevision','updatedAt','manualApplyReceipts','aiApiUsage','notifications','noticeCenter','lastNewBadgeRefresh','rpcmNewEntries','aiMemoryAutomation'])delete data[key];return JSON.stringify(data);};
    if(prior?.hash===hash||prior?.data&&content(prior.data)===content(data))return;
    const envelope={format:'RPCM_RESCUE_V1',origin:location.origin,kind,id,at:nowIso(),hash,data};const serialized=JSON.stringify(envelope);
    await GM_setValue(key,serialized);if(await GM_getValue(key,null)!==serialized)throw new Error('비상 보관본 저장 확인 실패');
    // Keep one previous content snapshot per entry, not hundreds of autosave versions.
    if(prior?.format==='RPCM_RESCUE_V1'&&prior.origin===location.origin&&prior.hash!==hash)await GM_setValue(rescueKey('previous-'+kind,id),JSON.stringify(prior));
    let entries=[];try{entries=JSON.parse(await GM_getValue(rescueKey('index'),'[]'));}catch(_){}
    entries=entries.filter(row=>!(row.kind===kind&&row.id===id));entries.push({kind,id,at:envelope.at,label:String(value.label||value.title||value.name||id)});
    await GM_setValue(rescueKey('index'),JSON.stringify(entries));rescueRuntime.error='';
  }
  function flushRescueCopies(){
    clearTimeout(rescueRuntime.timer);rescueRuntime.timer=null;
    const entries=[...rescueRuntime.pending.values()];rescueRuntime.pending.clear();
    rescueRuntime.queue=rescueRuntime.queue.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{
      for(const {kind,id} of entries){try{const stored=kind==='room'?await readManualStoredRoom({chatId:id}):await getCharacterLibrary(id);if(stored)await rescueStore(kind,stored);}catch(error){rescueRuntime.error=String(error.message||error);}}
    });return rescueRuntime.queue;
  }
  function queueRescueCopy(kind,value){
    if(!rescueUseful(kind,value))return;
    const id=String(kind==='room'?value.chatId:value.scopeId);rescueRuntime.pending.set(kind+':'+id,{kind,id});
    clearTimeout(rescueRuntime.timer);rescueRuntime.timer=setTimeout(flushRescueCopies,500);
  }
  async function seedRescueCopies(){
    if(rescueRuntime.seeded)return;rescueRuntime.seeded=true;
    try{const [rooms,libraries]=await Promise.all([getAllRooms(),getAllCharacterLibraries()]);rescueRuntime.queue=rescueRuntime.queue.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{for(const room of rooms)await rescueStore('room',room);for(const library of libraries)await rescueStore('library',library);}).catch(error=>{rescueRuntime.error=String(error.message||error);});
      let index=[];try{index=JSON.parse(await GM_getValue(rescueKey('index'),'[]'));}catch(_){}
      const missing=index.filter(row=>!row.deletedAt&&(row.kind==='room'?!rooms.some(room=>String(room.chatId)===row.id):!libraries.some(lib=>String(lib.scopeId)===row.id)));
      if(missing.length)notify(`사이트 저장소에서 찾지 못한 비상 보관본 ${missing.length}개가 있습니다. 자료 관리 → 도구·복사·백업·복원 → 저장소 점검·복구에서 확인하세요.`,'warn',10000);}catch(error){rescueRuntime.error=error.message;}
  }
  async function rescueMarkDeleted(kind,id){
    await flushRescueCopies();
    rescueRuntime.queue=rescueRuntime.queue.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{let rows=[];try{rows=JSON.parse(await GM_getValue(rescueKey('index'),'[]'));}catch(_){}for(const row of rows)if(row.kind===kind&&row.id===String(id))row.deletedAt=nowIso();await GM_setValue(rescueKey('index'),JSON.stringify(rows));}).catch(error=>{rescueRuntime.error=error.message;});
    await rescueRuntime.queue;
  }
  async function rescuePayload(previous=false){
    await flushRescueCopies();let entries=[];try{entries=JSON.parse(await GM_getValue(rescueKey('index'),'[]'));}catch(_){}
    const rooms=[],characterLibraries=[],errors=[];
    for(const row of entries){try{let raw=await GM_getValue(rescueKey((previous?'previous-':'')+row.kind,row.id),null);if(!raw&&previous)raw=await GM_getValue(rescueKey(row.kind,row.id),null);if(!raw)continue;const entry=typeof raw==='string'?JSON.parse(raw):raw;
      if(entry.format!=='RPCM_RESCUE_V1'||entry.origin!==location.origin||entry.kind!==row.kind||entry.id!==row.id||entry.hash!==await longTermHash(JSON.stringify(entry.data)))throw new Error('보관본 무결성 확인 실패');
      if(row.kind==='room'&&String(entry.data.chatId)===row.id)rooms.push(entry.data);else if(row.kind==='library'&&String(entry.data.scopeId)===row.id)characterLibraries.push(entry.data);else throw new Error('식별자 불일치');
    }catch(error){errors.push(row.label+': '+error.message);}}
    return {_rpContextManagerBackup:true,version:APP.version,exportedAt:nowIso(),rooms,characterLibraries,errors};
  }
  async function restoreRescueSelection(){
    const data=await rescuePayload(),[rooms,libraries]=await Promise.all([getAllRooms(),getAllCharacterLibraries()]);
    if(!data.rooms.length&&!data.characterLibraries.length)throw new Error('복구 가능한 비상 보관본이 없습니다. 다른 브라우저·프로필·사이트 주소인지 확인하세요.');
    const choice=await openBackupImportDialog(data,rooms,libraries);if(!choice)return;
    if(choice.mode==='clone-current')throw new Error('비상 복구는 원래 방으로 복원하는 모드를 선택하세요. 다른 방 복사는 백업 JSON 불러오기를 사용하세요.');
    // Preserve a downloadable pre-restore copy, including existing libraries.
    downloadText(JSON.stringify({_rpContextManagerBackup:true,version:APP.version,exportedAt:nowIso(),rooms,characterLibraries:libraries},null,2),'RP_비상복구_적용전_백업.json');
    for(const id of choice.roomIds){const source=data.rooms.find(r=>String(r.chatId)===String(id));if(!source)continue;const room=structuredClone(source);room.pending=null;room._rpcmRevision=Number(rooms.find(r=>String(r.chatId)===String(id))?._rpcmRevision||0);await saveRestoredRoom(room);}
    for(const id of choice.libraryIds){const lib=data.characterLibraries.find(l=>String(l.scopeId)===String(id));if(lib)await saveCharacterLibrary(structuredClone(lib));}
    await ensureCurrentRoom(getChatIdFromPath(),true);renderModalIfOpen();notify('선택한 방·설정집 비상 복구 완료','success');
  }
  function openStorageRecovery(){
    document.querySelector('#rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog')?.remove();const backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.id='rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog';
    backdrop.innerHTML=`<section class="rpcm-update-dialog rpcm-full-rebuild-dialog rpcm-storage-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="저장소 점검·복구"><header><div><h2>저장소 점검·복구</h2></div><button class="rpcm-update-dialog-close" aria-label="닫기">${rpcmUiIcon('close')}</button></header><div class="rpcm-update-result-body"><p>RP와 설정집은 이 브라우저의 사이트 저장소에 저장됩니다. 같은 브라우저·프로필·사이트 주소인지 먼저 확인하세요. 시크릿 모드, 사이트 데이터 삭제·브라우저 저장소 정리로 자료가 사라질 수 있습니다.</p><p>비상 보관본은 사용자 스크립트 관리자의 별도 저장공간에 최근 내용과 직전 내용을 보관합니다. 사이트 데이터가 없어져도 확인할 수 있지만, 브라우저 프로필·확장 프로그램 데이터까지 삭제되면 복구할 수 없습니다. 기기 밖에 보관할 전체 백업 JSON도 주기적으로 받아두세요.</p><p data-storage-status role="status">저장소 확인 중…</p><div class="rpcm-update-actions"><button class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-storage-persist>자료 보존 요청</button><button class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-storage-export>비상 보관본 JSON 받기</button><button class="rpcm-btn secondary" data-storage-previous>직전 보관본 JSON 받기</button><button class="rpcm-btn primary" data-storage-restore>복구할 자료 선택</button></div></div><footer><button class="rpcm-btn secondary rpcm-update-dialog-close">닫기</button></footer></section>`;
    const status=backdrop.querySelector('[data-storage-status]');const refresh=async()=>{try{await seedRescueCopies();const [data,rooms,libs,persisted,estimate]=await Promise.all([rescuePayload(),getAllRooms(),getAllCharacterLibraries(),navigator.storage?.persisted?.(),navigator.storage?.estimate?.()]);status.textContent=`현재 RP ${rooms.length}개 · 설정집 ${libs.length}개 / 비상 RP ${data.rooms.length}개 · 설정집 ${data.characterLibraries.length}개 · 브라우저 보존 ${persisted?'승인됨':'미승인'}${estimate?.usage?` · 사이트 사용량 ${Math.round(estimate.usage/1024/1024)}MB`:''}${data.errors.length?' · 무결성 오류 '+data.errors.length+'개':''}${rescueRuntime.error?' · 비상 저장 실패: '+rescueRuntime.error:''}`;}catch(error){status.textContent='저장소 확인 실패: '+error.message;}};
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-storage-persist]').onclick=async()=>{try{if(!navigator.storage?.persist)throw new Error('이 브라우저에서 지원하지 않습니다.');const allowed=await navigator.storage.persist();await refresh();if(!allowed)status.textContent+=' · 보존 요청을 브라우저가 승인하지 않았습니다.';}catch(error){status.textContent=error.message;}};
    for(const [attr,prior] of [['export',false],['previous',true]])backdrop.querySelector(`[data-storage-${attr}]`).onclick=async()=>{try{const data=await rescuePayload(prior);downloadText(JSON.stringify(data,null,2),`RP_비상보관본_${prior?'직전':'최근'}.json`);await refresh();}catch(error){status.textContent=error.message;}};
    backdrop.querySelector('[data-storage-restore]').onclick=async event=>{const button=event.currentTarget;button.disabled=true;backdrop.style.visibility='hidden';try{await restoreRescueSelection();await refresh();}catch(error){status.textContent='복구 실패: '+error.message;}finally{backdrop.style.visibility='';button.disabled=false;}};
    const close=()=>backdrop.remove();backdrop.querySelectorAll('.rpcm-update-dialog-close').forEach(b=>b.onclick=close);backdrop.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===backdrop)close();};backdrop.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')close();};document.body.append(backdrop);void refresh();
  }

  function traceRoomWrite(room,writer,status,expectedRevision,actualRevision,hash='',details={}) {
    if(status==='committed'||status==='readback-verified'){try{rpcmNewBadgeCommitted(room,details.before,writer);queueRescueCopy('room',room);}catch(_){}}
    try {
      const key='RPCM_room_write_trace_v1',rows=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]'),at=nowIso();
      const before=details.before,after=details.after||room;
      const changedKeys=before?Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(before),...Object.keys(after||{})])).filter(key=>JSON.stringify(before[key])!==JSON.stringify(after?.[key])):[];
      rows.push({at,roomId:String(room?.chatId||''),writer,status,expectedRevision,actualRevision,beforeRevision:expectedRevision,afterRevision:actualRevision,startedAt:details.startedAt||at,committedAt:status==='committed'||status==='readback-verified'?at:null,changedKeys,staleRejected:status==='stale-rejected',hash:String(hash||'')});
      localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(rows.slice(-200)));
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  function rpcmRoomClone(value){return value===undefined?undefined:structuredClone(value);}
  function rpcmRoomSig(has,value){if(!has)return '__RPCM_MISSING__';try{return JSON.stringify(value);}catch(_){return String(value);}}
  function rpcmRoomPlain(value){return !!value&&typeof value==='object'&&!Array.isArray(value)&&Object.getPrototypeOf(value)===Object.prototype;}
  function rpcmRoomArrayKey(rows){
    if(!rows.every(row=>row&&typeof row==='object'&&!Array.isArray(row)))return '';
    for(const key of ['id','cardId','slotId','jobId','key','ref']){
      const values=rows.map(row=>row?.[key]).filter(value=>value!==undefined&&value!==null&&String(value)!=='').map(String);
      if(values.length===rows.length&&new Set(values).size===values.length)return key;
    }
    return '';
  }
  function rpcmThreeWayNode(baseHas,base,localHas,local,remoteHas,remote,path,conflicts){
    const b=rpcmRoomSig(baseHas,base),l=rpcmRoomSig(localHas,local),r=rpcmRoomSig(remoteHas,remote);
    if(l===b)return remoteHas?{has:true,value:rpcmRoomClone(remote)}:{has:false};
    if(r===b)return localHas?{has:true,value:rpcmRoomClone(local)}:{has:false};
    if(l===r)return localHas?{has:true,value:rpcmRoomClone(local)}:{has:false};
    if((baseHas?rpcmRoomPlain(base):true)&&(localHas?rpcmRoomPlain(local):true)&&(remoteHas?rpcmRoomPlain(remote):true)){
      const out={},baseObj=baseHas?base:{},localObj=localHas?local:{},remoteObj=remoteHas?remote:{};
      const keys=new Set([...Object.keys(baseObj),...Object.keys(localObj),...Object.keys(remoteObj)]);
      for(const key of keys){
        if(!path&&['_rpcmRevision','updatedAt'].includes(key)){if(Object.hasOwn(remoteObj,key))out[key]=rpcmRoomClone(remoteObj[key]);continue;}
        const child=rpcmThreeWayNode(Object.hasOwn(baseObj,key),baseObj[key],Object.hasOwn(localObj,key),localObj[key],Object.hasOwn(remoteObj,key),remoteObj[key],path?`${path}.${key}`:key,conflicts);
        if(child.has)out[key]=child.value;
      }
      return {has:true,value:out};
    }
    if(baseHas&&localHas&&remoteHas&&Array.isArray(base)&&Array.isArray(local)&&Array.isArray(remote)){
      const all=[...base,...local,...remote],key=rpcmRoomArrayKey(all);
      if(key){
        const bm=new Map(base.map(row=>[String(row[key]),row])),lm=new Map(local.map(row=>[String(row[key]),row])),rm=new Map(remote.map(row=>[String(row[key]),row]));
        const order=[...remote.map(row=>String(row[key])),...local.map(row=>String(row[key])).filter(id=>!rm.has(id))],out=[];
        for(const id of order){
          const child=rpcmThreeWayNode(bm.has(id),bm.get(id),lm.has(id),lm.get(id),rm.has(id),rm.get(id),`${path}[${key}=${id}]`,conflicts);
          if(child.has)out.push(child.value);
        }
        return {has:true,value:out};
      }
    }
    conflicts.push(path||'(root)');
    return remoteHas?{has:true,value:rpcmRoomClone(remote)}:{has:false};
  }
  function rpcmRememberRoomBaseline(room){
    if(!room?.chatId)return;
    state.roomSaveBaselines ||= new Map();
    state.roomSaveBaselines.set(String(room.chatId),{revision:Number(room._rpcmRevision||0),snapshot:rpcmRoomClone(room)});
  }
  function rpcmRebaseRoomOntoStored(room,stored){
    const key=String(room?.chatId||''),entry=state.roomSaveBaselines?.get(key),priorRevision=Number(room?._rpcmRevision||0);
    if(!entry||entry.revision!==priorRevision)return {ok:false,reason:'기준 저장본이 없어 자동 병합할 수 없습니다.',conflicts:[]};
    const conflicts=[],merged=rpcmThreeWayNode(true,entry.snapshot,true,room,true,stored,'',conflicts).value;
    if(conflicts.length)return {ok:false,reason:`동시에 수정된 항목이 있습니다: ${conflicts.slice(0,4).join(', ')}${conflicts.length>4?' 외':''}`,conflicts};
    return {ok:true,room:merged,conflicts:[]};
  }
  function rpcmReplaceRoomObject(target,source){
    for(const key of Object.keys(target))if(!Object.hasOwn(source,key))delete target[key];
    const next=rpcmRoomClone(source);
    // Open editors retain card references. Keep them across commits.
    for (const field of ['slots','knowledgeCards','personStateRecords']) {
      const previous = new Map((target[field] || []).map(row => [String(row.id), row]));
      if (!Array.isArray(next[field])) continue;
      next[field] = next[field].map(row => {
        const existing = previous.get(String(row.id));
        if (!existing) return row;
        for (const key of Object.keys(existing)) if (!Object.hasOwn(row, key)) delete existing[key];
        Object.assign(existing, row);
        return existing;
      });
    }
    if(rpcmRoomPlain(target.longTermMemory)&&rpcmRoomPlain(next.longTermMemory))next.longTermMemory=longTermReconcileObject(target.longTermMemory,next.longTermMemory);
    Object.assign(target,next);
    return target;
  }

  function rpcmKeepLateEdits(before,live,stored){
    if(rpcmRoomSig(true,before)===rpcmRoomSig(true,live))return rpcmRoomClone(stored);
    if(rpcmRoomPlain(before)&&rpcmRoomPlain(live)&&rpcmRoomPlain(stored)){
      const out=rpcmRoomClone(stored);
      for(const key of new Set([...Object.keys(before),...Object.keys(live)])){
        if(['_rpcmRevision','updatedAt'].includes(key))continue;
        const bh=Object.hasOwn(before,key),lh=Object.hasOwn(live,key);
        if(rpcmRoomSig(bh,before[key])===rpcmRoomSig(lh,live[key]))continue;
        if(!lh)delete out[key];else out[key]=bh&&Object.hasOwn(stored,key)?rpcmKeepLateEdits(before[key],live[key],stored[key]):rpcmRoomClone(live[key]);
      }
      return out;
    }
    if(Array.isArray(before)&&Array.isArray(live)&&Array.isArray(stored)){
      const key=rpcmRoomArrayKey(before)||rpcmRoomArrayKey(live)||rpcmRoomArrayKey(stored);
      if(key&&[before,live,stored].every(rows=>!rows.length||rpcmRoomArrayKey(rows)===key)){
        const bm=new Map(before.map(row=>[String(row[key]),row])),lm=new Map(live.map(row=>[String(row[key]),row]));
        const out=stored.filter(row=>!bm.has(String(row[key]))||lm.has(String(row[key]))).map(row=>{
          const id=String(row[key]);return bm.has(id)&&lm.has(id)?rpcmKeepLateEdits(bm.get(id),lm.get(id),row):row;
        });
        const ids=new Set(out.map(row=>String(row[key])));
        for(const row of live)if(!bm.has(String(row[key]))&&!ids.has(String(row[key])))out.push(rpcmRoomClone(row));
        return out;
      }
    }
    return rpcmRoomClone(live);
  }

  async function saveRoom(room,writer='saveRoom',validateBeforeWrite=null) {
    if(writer==='saveRoom')writer=String(new Error().stack?.split('\n')[2]?.match(/at ([^ (]+)/)?.[1]||writer);
    const key=String(room.chatId||'');
    state.roomSaveQueues ||= new Map();
    const previous=state.roomSaveQueues.get(key)||Promise.resolve();
    const run=previous.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{
      const manualCommitLock=manualUpdateRuntime.commitLocks.get(key);
      if(manualCommitLock)await manualCommitLock;
      const stampedEdits=await stampManualExplicitEdits(room);
      let committedRoom=null,writtenLocal=null,storedForTrace=null,staleReason='';
      const priorRevision=Number(room._rpcmRevision||0),priorSnapshot=rpcmRoomClone(room),startedAt=nowIso();
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const tx=state.db.transaction(APP.storeName,'readwrite'),store=tx.objectStore(APP.storeName),get=store.get(room.chatId);
        let abortedForStale=false,validationError=null;
        get.onsuccess=()=>{
          const stored=get.result;storedForTrace=stored||{};
          const revision=Number(stored?._rpcmRevision||0);
          let candidate=rpcmRoomClone(room);writtenLocal=rpcmRoomClone(candidate);
          if(stored&&rescueUseful('room',stored)&&!rescueUseful('room',candidate)&&!state.roomSaveBaselines?.has(key)&&writer!=='backup-restore'){staleReason='빈 초기 데이터로 기존 기억을 덮어쓰려는 저장을 차단했습니다.';abortedForStale=true;tx.abort();return;}
          if(priorRevision!==revision){
            if(!stored){staleReason='저장본이 사라져 자동 병합할 수 없습니다.';abortedForStale=true;tx.abort();return;}
            const rebased=rpcmRebaseRoomOntoStored(room,stored);
            if(!rebased.ok){staleReason=rebased.reason;traceRoomWrite(room,writer,'stale-rejected',priorRevision,revision,'',{startedAt,before:stored,after:room});abortedForStale=true;tx.abort();return;}
            candidate=rebased.room;
            traceRoomWrite(candidate,writer,'stale-rebased',priorRevision,revision,'',{startedAt,before:stored,after:candidate});
          }
          try { if (validateBeforeWrite) validateBeforeWrite(candidate); }
          catch (error) { validationError=error;tx.abort();return; }
          candidate._rpcmRevision=revision+1;candidate.updatedAt=nowIso();committedRoom=candidate;store.put(candidate);
        };
        get.onerror=()=>tx.abort();
        tx.oncomplete=()=>resolve();
        tx.onabort=()=>reject(validationError || new Error(abortedForStale?`최신 저장본과 자동 병합하지 못했습니다. ${staleReason} 다른 탭에서 같은 항목을 수정했다면 최신 화면을 다시 열어 주세요.`:`IndexedDB 저장이 취소됐습니다.${tx.error?.name?` (${tx.error.name}: ${tx.error.message||''})`:''}`));
        tx.onerror=()=>{const error=tx.error||new Error('IndexedDB 저장 실패');const name=String(error?.name||'').toLowerCase(),message=String(error?.message||error||'');reject(/quota/.test(name+' '+message.toLowerCase())?new Error(`브라우저 IndexedDB 저장공간 한도를 초과했습니다. RP Manager 데이터가 너무 커졌거나 브라우저가 이 사이트 저장공간을 제한하고 있습니다. (${message||error.name})`):error);};
      });
      if(!committedRoom)throw new Error('저장 결과를 만들지 못했습니다.');
      rpcmReplaceRoomObject(room,rpcmKeepLateEdits(writtenLocal,room,committedRoom));rpcmRememberRoomBaseline(committedRoom);
      try{queueSemanticIndexRefresh(room,'saved');}catch(error){console.warn('[Wish semantic] index scheduling',error);}
      traceRoomWrite(room,writer,'committed',priorRevision,room._rpcmRevision,'',{startedAt,before:storedForTrace||{},after:room});
      const editTasks=manualExplicitEdits.get(key);for(const [task,version] of stampedEdits)if(editTasks?.get(task)===version)editTasks.delete(task);
      if(room.chatId===state.currentChatId){state.lastSavedAt=Date.now();updateSaveStatusUi('saved');}
    });
    state.roomSaveQueues.set(key,run);
    try{return await run;}finally{if(state.roomSaveQueues.get(key)===run)state.roomSaveQueues.delete(key);}
  }

  async function getAllRooms() {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const tx = state.db.transaction(APP.storeName, 'readonly');
      const req = tx.objectStore(APP.storeName).getAll();
      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || []);
      req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
    });
  }
